local a = (function()
    local a = game:GetService("TweenService")

    k.Flags = {}
    k.Windows = {}
    local function l(m, n)
        local o = {}
        if type(m) == "string" then
            o.Name = m
        elseif type(m) == "table" then
            for p, q in pairs(m) do
                o[p] = q
            end
        end
        for p, q in pairs(n or {}) do
            if o[q] == nil and o[p] ~= nil then
                o[q] = o[p]
            end
        end
        if o.Range then
            o.Min = o.Min or o.Range[1]
            o.Max = o.Max or o.Range[2]
        end
        return o
    end
    local function m(n, o, p, q, r)
        p._type = r
        p._frame = q
        p._listeners = p._listeners or {}
        local s = n and n.Window
        local t = o.Name or o.Text or o.Title
        p._searchName = type(t) == "string" and t or ""
        p._searchKey = string.lower(p._searchName)
        if n and n._items then
            table.insert(n._items, p)
        end
        if o.Flag then
            k.Flags[o.Flag] = p
            if s then
                s:_flagCreated(o.Flag, p)
            end
        end
        p._userVisible = o.Visible ~= false
        function p:IsVisible()
            return p._userVisible
        end
        function p:_isShown()
            return p._userVisible and not (n and n._userVisible == false)
        end
        function p:SetVisible(u)
            u = u and true or false
            if u == p._userVisible or p._destroyed then
                return
            end
            p._userVisible = u
            if not u then
                if p._onHide then
                    p._onHide()
                end
                if p.Open and type(p.SetOpen) == "function" then
                    p:SetOpen(false)
                end
            end
            if q then
                q.Visible = u
            end
            if s then
                s._controlsDirty = true
            end
        end
        if q and not p._userVisible then
            q.Visible = false
        end
        function p:SetTooltip(u)
            p._tooltip = u
            if q and s and not p._tooltipBound then
                p._tooltipBound = true
                s:_attachTooltip(p, q)
            end
        end
        function p:GetTooltip()
            return p._tooltip
        end
        if o.Tooltip ~= nil and o.Tooltip ~= false then
            p:SetTooltip(o.Tooltip)
        end
        function p:Destroy()
            if p._destroyed then
                return
            end
            p._destroyed = true
            if s and s._tipOwner == p then
                s:_hideTooltip()
            end
            for u, v in ipairs(p._listeners) do
                v()
            end
            p._listeners = {}
            if n and n._items then
                local u = table.find(n._items, p)
                if u then
                    table.remove(n._items, u)
                end
            end
            if o.Flag and k.Flags[o.Flag] == p then
                k.Flags[o.Flag] = nil
            end
            if s then
                s._controlsDirty = true
            end
            if q then
                q:Destroy()
            end
        end
        return p
    end

        }
    end

    local q = [[https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Footagesus/Icons/refs/heads/main/lucide/dist/Icons.lua]]
    local r = nil
    local function s()
        if r ~= nil then
            return r
        end
        local t, u = pcall(function()
            local t = game:HttpGet(q)
            return loadstring(t)()
        end)
        if t and type(u) == "table" then
            r = u
        else
            r = false
            warn("[AirFlow] lucide icons unavailable: " .. tostring(u))
        end
        return r
    end
    local t = {
        ValleySans = {
            Regular = [[https://raw.githubusercontent.com/HelsinkiTypeStudio/valley-sans/main/fonts/ttf/ValleySans-Regular.ttf]],
            Medium = [[https://raw.githubusercontent.com/HelsinkiTypeStudio/valley-sans/main/fonts/ttf/ValleySans-Medium.ttf]],
            SemiBold = [[https://raw.githubusercontent.com/HelsinkiTypeStudio/valley-sans/main/fonts/ttf/ValleySans-SemiBold.ttf]],
        },
    }
    local u = {
        Regular = { 400, Enum.FontWeight.Regular },
        Medium = { 500, Enum.FontWeight.Medium },
        SemiBold = { 600, Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold },
        Bold = { 700, Enum.FontWeight.Bold },
    }
    function k:LoadFont(v)
        v = l(v, {})
        if type(writefile) ~= "function" or type(isfile) ~= "function" or typeof(getcustomasset) ~= "function" then
            warn([[[AirFlow] custom fonts need writefile, isfile and getcustomasset]])
            return false
        end
        local w = v.Name or "CustomFont"
        local x = v.Weights or t[w]
        if type(x) ~= "table" then
            warn("[AirFlow] no font weights for " .. w)
            return false
        end
        local y = v.Folder or "AirFlowFonts"
        pcall(function()
            if type(isfolder) == "function" and type(makefolder) == "function" and not isfolder(y) then
                makefolder(y)
            end
        end)
        local z = {}
        for A, B in pairs(x) do
            local C = u[A]
            if C then
                local D = y .. "/" .. w .. "-" .. A .. ".ttf"
                local E = true
                if not isfile(D) then
                    E = pcall(function()
                        writefile(D, game:HttpGet(B))
                    end)
                end
                if E then
                    table.insert(z, { name = A, weight = C[1], style = "normal", assetId = getcustomasset(D) })
                else
                    warn("[AirFlow] could not download " .. A .. " weight of " .. w)
                end
            end
        end
        if #z == 0 then
            return false
        end
        local A = y .. "/" .. w .. ".json"
        local B = pcall(function()
            writefile(A, e:JSONEncode({ name = w, faces = z }))
        end)
        if not B then
            return false
        end
        local C = getcustomasset(A)
        local function D(E, F)
            local G = u[E]
            if G and x[E] then
                return Font.new(C, G[2])
            end
            return F
        end
        k.Fonts.Regular = D("Regular", k.Fonts.Regular)
        k.Fonts.Medium = D("Medium", D("Regular", k.Fonts.Medium))
        k.Fonts.Bold = D("SemiBold", D("Bold", k.Fonts.Bold))
        return true
    end
    function k:PreloadIcons()
        return s() ~= false
    end
    local function v(w)
        if type(w) == "number" then
            return "rbxassetid://" .. string.format("%.0f", w)
        end
        if type(w) == "string" then
            if w:match("^%d+$") then
                return "rbxassetid://" .. w
            end
            if w:find("^rbxassetid://") or w:find("^rbxasset://") or w:find("^rbxthumb://") or w:find("^http") then
                return w
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function w(x)
        if x == k.Assets.Logo and k._logoUrl ~= nil then
            x = k._logoUrl or "wind"
        end
        if typeof(x) == "table" then
            return v(x.Image) or x.Image, x.RectOffset, x.RectSize, true, x.Tint == true
        end
        local y = v(x)
        if y then
            return y, nil, nil, true, false
        end
        if type(x) ~= "string" then
            return nil
        end
        local z = x:gsub("^lucide:", "")
        local A = s()
        if not A then
            return nil
        end
        local B = (A.Icons and A.Icons[z]) or A[z]
        if type(B) == "table" then
            local C = B.Image
            if type(C) == "number" then
                C = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(C)
            end
            local D = (A.Spritesheets and A.Spritesheets[tostring(C)]) or C
            return D, B.ImageRectPosition, B.ImageRectSize
        elseif type(B) == "string" then
            return B
        end
        warn("[AirFlow] unknown lucide icon: " .. z)
        return nil
    end
    local x = "chevron-right"
    local y = "rbxasset://fonts/families/BuilderSans.json"
    k.Fonts = {
        Regular = Font.new(y, Enum.FontWeight.Regular),
        Medium = Font.new(y, Enum.FontWeight.Medium),
        Bold = Font.new(y, Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold),
    }
    local z = k.Theme
    local A = k.Assets
    local D = k.Fonts
    local E = b.TouchEnabled and not b.MouseEnabled
    k.Touch = E
    local F = 188
    local G = 72
    local H = 34
    local I = E and 54 or 48
    local J = E and 70 or 64
    local K = E and 40 or 34
    local L = E and 34 or 28
    local M = 64
    local N = 176
    local O = 440
    local P = {
        Card = {

    local Q = {}
    local R = {}
    local S = {}
    local T = {}
    local U = {}
    local V = 0
    local W = 4000
    local function X(Y, Z)
        for _ in pairs(Y) do
            if _.Parent == nil then
                if U[_] then
                    Y[_] = nil
                    U[_] = nil
                    if Z then
                        V -= 1
                    end
                else
                    U[_] = true
                end
            else
                U[_] = nil
            end
        end
    end
    local function Y()
        X(R, true)
        X(S, false)
        W = math.max(4000, V * 2)
    end
    local function Z(_)
        return math.floor(_.R * 255 + 0.5) .. "," .. math.floor(_.G * 255 + 0.5) .. "," .. math.floor(_.B * 255 + 0.5)
    end
    local function _()
        table.clear(T)
        for aa = #n, 1, -1 do
            local ab = n[aa]
            T[Z(k.Theme[ab])] = ab
        end
    end
    _()
    local function aa(ab, ac)
        for ad, ae in pairs(ac) do
            if typeof(ae) == "Color3" then
                local af = T[Z(ae)]
                local ag = R[ab]
                if af then
                    if not ag then
                        ag = {}
                        R[ab] = ag
                        V += 1
                        if V >= W then
                            Y()
                        end
                    end
                    ag[ad] = af
                elseif ag then
                    ag[ad] = nil
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local ab = table.clone(k.Theme)
    local ac = nil
    local function ad(ae, af, ag)
        for ah, ai in pairs(af) do
            local aj = ag[ai]
            if aj then
                ae[ah] = aj
            end
        end
    end
    local function ae(af)
        for ag, ah in pairs(R) do
            pcall(ad, ag, ah, af)
        end
    end
    local function af(ag)
        if ac then
            ac:Disconnect()
            ac = nil
        end
        local ah, ai = {}, {}
        for aj, ak in ipairs(n) do
            local al = k.Theme[ak]
            local am = ab[ak] or al
            if am ~= al then
                ah[ak] = am
                ai[ak] = am
            end
        end
        local function aj()
            for ak, al in ipairs(n) do
                ai[al] = k.Theme[al]
                ab[al] = k.Theme[al]
            end
            ae(ai)
        end
        if ag <= 0 or next(ah) == nil then
            aj()
            return
        end
        local ak = 0
        ac = d.Heartbeat:Connect(function(al)
            ak += al
            if ak >= ag then
                ac:Disconnect()
                ac = nil
                aj()
                return
            end
            local am = 1 - (1 - ak / ag) ^ 5
            for an, ao in pairs(ah) do
                local ap = ao:Lerp(k.Theme[an], am)
                ai[an] = ap
                ab[an] = ap
            end
            ae(ai)
        end)
    end
    local function ag(ah, ai)
        S[ah] = ai
        ai()
    end
    local function ah(ai, aj, ak, al, am, an)
        ak = ak or 0.2
        if not an then
            aa(ai, aj)
        end
        if ak <= 0 then
            for ao, ap in pairs(aj) do
                ai[ao] = ap
            end
            return nil
        end
        al = al or Enum.EasingStyle.Quart
        am = am or Enum.EasingDirection.Out
        local ao = ak .. al.Name .. am.Name
        local ap = Q[ao]
        if not ap then
            ap = TweenInfo.new(ak, al, am)
            Q[ao] = ap
        end
        local aq = a:Create(ai, ap, aj)
        aq:Play()
        return aq
    end
    local function ai(aj, ak, al)
        local am = Instance.new(aj)
        for an, ao in pairs(ak) do
            if an ~= "Parent" then
                am[an] = ao
            end
        end
        aa(am, ak)
        if al then
            for an, ao in ipairs(al) do
                ao.Parent = am
            end
        end
        if ak.Parent then
            am.Parent = ak.Parent
        end
        return am
    end
    k.Roundness = 1
    k._cornerBases = {}
    local function aj(ak, al)
        al = al or UDim.new(0, 8)
        local am = ai(
            "UICorner",
            { CornerRadius = al.Scale == 0 and UDim.new(0, math.floor(al.Offset * k.Roundness + 0.5)) or al, Parent = ak }
        )
        if al.Scale == 0 then
            k._cornerBases[am] = al.Offset
        end
        return am
    end
    function k._fixedCorner(ak, al)
        return ai("UICorner", { CornerRadius = al, Parent = ak })
    end
    function k._radius(ak)
        return math.floor(ak * k.Roundness + 0.5)
    end
    local function ak(al, am, an, ao)
        return ai(
            "UIStroke",
            {
                Color = am or z.Stroke,
                Transparency = an or 0,
                Thickness = ao or 1,
                ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
                Parent = al,
            }
        )
    end
    local function al(am, an, ao, ap, aq)
        return ai(
            "UIPadding",
            {
                PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, an or 0),
                PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, ao or 0),
                PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, ap or 0),
                PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, aq or 0),
                Parent = am,
            }
        )
    end
    local function am(an)
        local ao = {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            TextColor3 = z.Text,
            TextSize = 14,
            FontFace = D.Medium,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        }
        for ap, aq in pairs(an) do
            ao[ap] = aq
        end
        return ai("TextLabel", ao)
    end
    local function an(ao, ap, aq, ar, as)
        local at = ai(
            "ImageLabel",
            {
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
                Position = aq,
                Size = ap,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Image = A.Glow,
                ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(226, 218, 230),
                ImageTransparency = 1,
                Visible = false,
                ZIndex = 0,
                Parent = ao,
            }
        )
        local au = ai("UIGradient", { Rotation = as or 90, Parent = at })
        ag(au, function()
            au.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), z.Accent)
        end)
        return at
    end
    local function ao(ap)
        return ai(
            "Frame",
            {
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                ZIndex = 0,
                Parent = ap,
            }
        )
    end
    local ap = 1.35
    local function aq(ar, as, at)
        local au, av, aw, ax, ay = w(as)
        if not au then
            return
        end
        ar.Image = au
        ar.ImageRectOffset = av or Vector2.zero
        ar.ImageRectSize = aw or Vector2.zero
        if as == A.Logo then
            ay = true
            k._logoLabels[ar] = true
            ah(ar, { ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 254) }, 0)
        end
        local az = ax and not ay
        ar:SetAttribute("CustomIcon", az or nil)
        if az then
            ah(ar, { ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }, 0)
        end
        local aA = ax and not at and as ~= A.Logo and (typeof(as) == "table" and tonumber(as.Scale) or ap) or 1
        local aB = ar:FindFirstChild("CustomIconScale")
        if aA ~= 1 then
            aB = aB or ai("UIScale", { Name = "CustomIconScale", Parent = ar })
            aB.Scale = aA
        elseif aB then
            aB:Destroy()
        end
    end
    local function logoApply()
        local img, off, size = w(k.Assets.Logo)
        if not img then
            return
        end
        for lbl in pairs(k._logoLabels) do
            if lbl.Parent then
                lbl.Image = img
                lbl.ImageRectOffset = off or Vector2.zero
                lbl.ImageRectSize = size or Vector2.zero
            end
        end
    end
    function k._resolveLogo(host)
        if k._logoState then
            return k._logoState
        end
        local state = { Done = false }
        k._logoState = state
        task.spawn(function()
            local t0 = os.clock()
            while host and not host.Parent and os.clock() - t0 < 3 do
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
            local okCP, cp = pcall(function()
                return game:GetService("ContentProvider")
            end)
            local probe = ai("ImageLabel", {
                Name = "LogoProbe",
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-8, -8),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 2),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                ImageTransparency = 0.98,
                Parent = host,
            })
            local weak
            for _, src in ipairs(k.LogoSources) do
                probe.Image = src
                local status
                if okCP then
                    pcall(function()
                        cp:PreloadAsync({ probe }, function(_, st)
                            status = st
                        end)
                    end)
                end
                if status ~= Enum.AssetFetchStatus.Failure and status ~= Enum.AssetFetchStatus.TimedOut then
                    local t1 = os.clock()
                    while not probe.IsLoaded and os.clock() - t1 < 1.5 do
                        task.wait(0.05)
                    end
                    if probe.IsLoaded then
                        k._logoUrl = src
                        break
                    end
                    if status == Enum.AssetFetchStatus.Success then
                        weak = weak or src
                    end
                end
            end
            if k._logoUrl == nil then
                k._logoUrl = weak or false
                if not weak then
                    warn("[AirFlow] logo could not be loaded, using fallback icon")
                end
            end
            probe:Destroy()
            state.Done = true
            logoApply()
        end)
        return state
    end
    local function ar(as, at, au, av)
        if as:GetAttribute("CustomIcon") then
            ah(as, { ImageTransparency = au and 0 or 0.4 }, av)
        else
            ah(as, { ImageColor3 = at }, av)
        end
    end
    local function as()
        local at
        pcall(function()
            if typeof(gethui) == "function" then
                at = gethui()
            end
        end)
        if typeof(at) == "Instance" then
            return at
        end
        local au
        pcall(function()
            au = game:GetService("CoreGui")
            local av = Instance.new("Folder")
            av.Parent = au
            av:Destroy()
        end)
        if au then
            return au
        end
        return j:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    end
    local at, au = nil, Vector2.zero
    local function av()
        local aw = time()
        if at ~= aw then
            at = aw
            au = c:GetGuiInset()
        end
        return b:GetMouseLocation() - au
    end
    local function aw(ax)
        return ax.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or ax.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
    end
    local function ax()
        return b:GetLastInputType() == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
    end
    local function ay(az, aA)
        local aB
        az.InputBegan:Connect(function(aC)
            if aw(aC) then
                local aD = aA()
                aB = aD and aD.CanvasPosition
            end
        end)
        return function()
            local aC = aA()
            return not (aB and aC and (aC.CanvasPosition - aB).Magnitude > 4)
        end
    end
    local function az(aA, aB)
        local aC, aD = aB.AbsolutePosition, aB.AbsoluteSize
        return aA.X >= aC.X and aA.X <= aC.X + aD.X and aA.Y >= aC.Y and aA.Y <= aC.Y + aD.Y
    end
    local function aA(aB)
        return aB.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or aB.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
    end
    local function aB(aC, aD, aE, aF)
        local aG = ai(
            "Frame",
            {
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
                Position = aF,
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Parent = aC,
            }
        )
        local aH = ai(
            "ImageLabel",
            { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ImageColor3 = aE, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit, Parent = aG }
        )
        aq(aH, aD)
        return aG, aH
    end
    local function aC(aD, aE, aF)
        local aG = ai(
            "Frame",
            {
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(aE, 0.5),
                Position = UDim2.new(aE, aF, 0.5, 0),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Parent = aD,
            }
        )
        local aH = {}
        local aI = ai(
            "ImageLabel",
            {
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
                Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                ImageColor3 = z.Muted,
                ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
                Parent = aG,
            }
        )
        aq(aI, "chevron-down")
        if aI.Image ~= "" then
            function aH:Set(aJ)
                ah(
                    aI,
                    { Rotation = aJ and 180 or 0, ImageColor3 = aJ and z.Accent or z.Muted },
                    0.3,
                    Enum.EasingStyle.Quint
                )
            end
            return aH
        end
        aI:Destroy()
        local function aJ(aK, aL)
            return am({
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
                Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18),
                Text = "\u{203a}",
                TextSize = 22,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
                TextColor3 = z.Muted,
                TextTransparency = aL,
                Rotation = aK,
                Parent = aG,
            })
        end
        local aK = aJ(90, 0)
        local aL = aJ(270, 1)
        function aH:Set(aM)
            ah(aK, { TextTransparency = aM and 1 or 0 }, 0.2)
            ah(aL, { TextTransparency = aM and 0 or 1, TextColor3 = aM and z.Accent or z.Muted }, 0.2)
        end
        return aH
    end
    local aD = {}
    local function aE(aF)
        local aG = av()
        local aH = math.max(aF.AbsoluteSize.X, aF.AbsoluteSize.Y) * 2.2
        local aI = table.remove(aD)
        if aI and not pcall(function()
            aI.Parent = nil
        end) then
            aI = nil
        end
        if not aI then
            aI = ai("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BackgroundColor3 = z.Accent, BorderSizePixel = 0 })
            aj(aI, UDim.new(1, 0))
        end
        aI.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(aG.X - aF.AbsolutePosition.X, aG.Y - aF.AbsolutePosition.Y)
        aI.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
        aI.BackgroundColor3 = z.Accent
        aI.BackgroundTransparency = 0.82
        aI.ZIndex = aF.ZIndex + 1
        aI.Parent = aF
        ah(aI, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(aH, aH), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.55)
        task.delay(0.55, function()
            if aI.Parent ~= aF or not aF.Parent then
                pcall(aI.Destroy, aI)
                return
            end
            aI.Parent = nil
            if #aD < 4 then
                table.insert(aD, aI)
            else
                aI:Destroy()
            end
        end)
    end
    local function aF(aG)
        if not aG or aG:GetAttribute("Hidden") then
            return
        end
        ah(aG, { Color = z.Accent, Transparency = 0.2 }, 0.08)
        task.delay(0.12, function()
            ah(aG, { Color = z.Stroke, Transparency = 0 }, 0.35)
        end)
    end
    local function aG(aH, aI)
        aH.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            ah(aI, { Color = z.StrokeHover }, 0.12)
        end)
        aH.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            ah(aI, { Color = z.Stroke }, 0.25)
        end)
    end
    local aH = {
        [Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl] = "LCtrl",
        [Enum.KeyCode.RightControl] = "RCtrl",
        [Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift] = "LShift",
        [Enum.KeyCode.RightShift] = "RShift",
        [Enum.KeyCode.LeftAlt] = "LAlt",
        [Enum.KeyCode.RightAlt] = "RAlt",
        [Enum.KeyCode.Return] = "Enter",
        [Enum.KeyCode.Escape] = "Esc",
        [Enum.KeyCode.Backspace] = "Backspace",
    }
    local function aI(aJ)
        if aJ == nil then
            return "None"
        end
        return aH[aJ] or aJ.Name
    end
    local function aJ(aK, ...)
        if type(aK) ~= "function" then
            return
        end
        local aL, aM = pcall(aK, ...)
        if not aL then
            warn("[AirFlow] callback error: " .. tostring(aM))
        end
    end
    local function aK(aL)
        local aM = nil
        task.spawn(function()
            aM = table.pack(pcall(aL))
        end)
        while aM == nil do
            task.wait()
        end
        return table.unpack(aM, 1, aM.n)
    end
    local function aL(aM, aN, aO)
        local function decimalsOf(n)
            local s = string.format("%.8f", math.abs(n)):gsub("0+$", "")
            local dot = s:find(".", 1, true)
            return dot and (#s - dot) or 0
        end
        local aP = math.min(math.max(decimalsOf(aO), decimalsOf(aM), decimalsOf(aN)), 8)
        local aS = "%." .. aP .. "f"
        local mult = 10 ^ aP
        return {
            snap = function(aT)
                aT = math.clamp(aT, aM, aN)
                local snapped = aM + math.floor((aT - aM) / aO + 0.5) * aO
                -- the end of the range stays reachable even when it is not on the step grid
                if snapped > aN or math.abs(aN - aT) < math.abs(snapped - aT) then
                    snapped = aN
                end
                snapped = math.floor(snapped * mult + 0.5) / mult
                return math.clamp(snapped == 0 and 0 or snapped, aM, aN)
            end,
            format = function(aT)
                return string.format(aS, aT)
            end,
        }
    end
    local function aM(aN)
        return aN._metrics or P.Card
    end
    local function aN(aO, aP)
        local aQ = aO.AbsoluteSize.X / aP.Scale.Scale
        return math.clamp(math.floor(aQ * 0.48), 90, 220)
    end
    local function aO(aP, aQ, aR, aS)
        local aT = aM(aP).Compact
        local aU = {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, aR),
            BackgroundColor3 = z.Surface2,
            BackgroundTransparency = aT and 1 or 0,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            LayoutOrder = aP:_nextOrder(),
            Parent = aP.List,
        }
        if aQ == "TextButton" then
            aU.AutoButtonColor = false
            aU.Text = ""
        end
        local aV = ai(aQ, aU)
        aV:SetAttribute("NoDrag", true)
        aj(aV)
        local aW = ak(aV, z.Stroke, aT and 1 or 0)
        if aT then
            aW:SetAttribute("Hidden", true)
        end
        return aV, aW
    end
    local function aP(aQ, aR, aS, aT, aU)
        aU = aU or P.Card
        local aV, aW
        if aS then
            local aX = math.floor((aU.DescHeight - (aU.DescGap + aU.DescLine)) / 2)
            aV = am({
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, aX),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -(14 + aT), 0, aU.TitleHeight),
                Text = aR,
                TextSize = aU.TextSize,
                Parent = aQ,
            })
            aW = am({
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, aX + aU.DescGap),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -(14 + aT), 0, aU.DescLine),
                Text = aS,
                TextSize = aU.DescSize,
                FontFace = D.Regular,
                TextColor3 = z.Muted,
                Parent = aQ,
            })
        else
            aV = am({
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -(14 + aT), 1, 0),
                Text = aR,
                TextSize = aU.TextSize,
                Parent = aQ,
            })
        end
        return aV, aW
    end
    local function aQ(aR, aS, aT, aU, aV, aW)
        aS = l(aS, aT)
        local aX = aM(aR)
        local aY = aS.Desc and aX.DescHeight or aX.Height
        local aZ, a_ = aO(aR, aU or "Frame", aY, aS)
        aG(aZ, a_)
        local a0, a1
        if aV then
            a0, a1 = aP(aZ, aS.Name or aW, aS.Desc, aV, aX)
        end
        return aS, aZ, a_, aY, a0, a1
    end
    local function aR(aS, aT, aU)
        if aS then
            aS.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(14 + aU), aS.Size.Y.Scale, aS.Size.Y.Offset)
        end
        if aT then
            aT.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(14 + aU), 0, aT.Size.Y.Offset)
        end
    end
    local aS = {}
    aS.__index = aS
    function aS:_nextOrder()
        self._order = self._order + 1
        return self._order
    end
    function aS:Section(aT)
        local aU = not (type(aT) == "table" and aT.Visible == false)
        if type(aT) == "table" then
            aT = aT.Name or aT.Title or ""
        end
        local aV = aM(self).Compact and 14 or 2
        local aW = ai(
            "Frame",
            { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = self:_nextOrder(), Parent = self.List }
        )
        local aX = am({
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(aV, 8),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 16),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
            Text = string.upper(aT),
            TextSize = 12,
            TextColor3 = z.Muted,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None,
            Parent = aW,
        })
        local aY = aV == 2 and 0 or aV
        local aZ = ai(
            "Frame",
            {
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -aY, 0, 16),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 1),
                BackgroundColor3 = z.Stroke,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Parent = aW,
            }
        )
        local function a_()
            aZ.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(aX.AbsoluteSize.X / self.Window.Scale.Scale + aV + 12 + aY), 0, 1)
        end
        aX:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(a_)
        task.defer(a_)
        return m(self, { Visible = aU }, {
            Set = function(a0, a1)
                aX.Text = string.upper(a1)
            end,
        }, aW, "Section")
    end
    function aS:Divider(aT)
        local aU = type(aT) == "table" and (aT.Text or aT.Name) or type(aT) == "string" and aT or nil
        if aU then
            local aV = aM(self).Compact and 14 or 2
            local aW = ai(
                "Frame",
                { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = self:_nextOrder(), Parent = self.List }
            )
            local aX = am({
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
                Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 14),
                AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
                Text = aU,
                TextSize = 11,
                FontFace = D.Regular,
                TextColor3 = z.Muted,
                TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None,
                Parent = aW,
            })
            al(aX, 8, 8)
            local aY = {}
            for aZ, a_ in ipairs({ 0, 1 }) do
                aY[aZ] = ai(
                    "Frame",
                    {
                        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(a_, 0.5),
                        Position = UDim2.new(a_, a_ == 0 and aV or -aV, 0.5, 0),
                        Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -aV, 0, 1),
                        BackgroundColor3 = z.Stroke,
                        BorderSizePixel = 0,
                        Parent = aW,
                    }
                )
            end
            local function aZ()
                local a_ = aX.AbsoluteSize.X / self.Window.Scale.Scale / 2
                for a0, a1 in ipairs(aY) do
                    a1.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -(aV + a_), 0, 1)
                end
            end
            aX:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(aZ)
            task.defer(aZ)
            return m(self, { Visible = not (type(aT) == "table" and aT.Visible == false) }, {
                Set = function(a_, a0)
                    aX.Text = tostring(a0)
                end,
            }, aW, "Divider")
        end
        local aV = ai(
            "Frame",
            {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
                BackgroundColor3 = z.Stroke,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                LayoutOrder = self:_nextOrder(),
                Parent = self.List,
            }
        )
        if aM(self).Compact then
            aV.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            aV.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 9)
            ai(
                "Frame",
                {
                    Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 4),
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 1),
                    BackgroundColor3 = z.Stroke,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    Parent = aV,
                }
            )
        end
        return m(self, { Visible = not (type(aT) == "table" and aT.Visible == false) }, {}, aV, "Divider")
    end
    function aS:Label(aT)
        aT = l(aT, { Name = "Text", Title = "Text" })
        local aU = am({
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18),
            Text = k._rich(aT.Text or ""),
            TextSize = 13,
            FontFace = D.Regular,
            TextColor3 = k._color(aT.Color) or z.Muted,
            RichText = true,
            LayoutOrder = self:_nextOrder(),
            Parent = self.List,
        })
        if aM(self).Compact then
            aU.TextSize = 12
            al(aU, 14, 14)
        else
            al(aU, 2)
        end
        local aV = {
            Set = function(aV, aW)
                aU.Text = k._rich(aW)
            end,
            Get = function()
                return aU.Text
            end,
            SetColor = function(aV, aW)
                ah(aU, { TextColor3 = k._color(aW) or z.Muted }, 0.25)
            end,
        }
        if type(aT.Update) == "function" then
            local aW = math.max(tonumber(aT.UpdateRate) or 1, 0.05)
            local aX = true
            aV._listeners = aV._listeners or {}
            table.insert(aV._listeners, function()
                aX = false
            end)
            task.spawn(function()
                while aX and aU.Parent do
                    local aY, aZ = aK(aT.Update)
                    if aY and aZ ~= nil then
                        aU.Text = k._rich(aZ)
                    elseif not aY then
                        warn("[AirFlow] label update error: " .. tostring(aZ))
                    end
                    task.wait(aW)
                end
            end)
            function aV:SetUpdateRate(aY)
                aW = math.max(tonumber(aY) or aW, 0.05)
            end
        end
        return m(self, aT, aV, aU, "Label")
    end
    function aS:Paragraph(aT)
        aT = l(aT, { Title = "Name" })
        local aU = aO(self, "Frame", 0, aT)
        aU.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        if aM(self).Compact then
            al(aU, 14, 14, 6, 8)
        else
            al(aU, 14, 14, 11, 12)
        end
        ai("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), Parent = aU })
        am({ Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14), Text = aT.Name or "", LayoutOrder = 1, Parent = aU })
        local aV = am({
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            Text = k._rich(aT.Content or ""),
            TextSize = 13,
            FontFace = D.Regular,
            TextColor3 = z.Muted,
            TextWrapped = true,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None,
            TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
            RichText = true,
            LayoutOrder = 2,
            Parent = aU,
        })
        return m(self, aT, {
            Set = function(aW, aX)
                aV.Text = k._rich(aX)
            end,
        }, aU, "Paragraph")
    end
    k._btnSpec = {
        chip = {
            Default = {
                { "Surface3", 0.45, "Stroke", 0 },
                { "Surface3", 0.2, "StrokeHover", 0 },
                { "Surface3", 0.05, "StrokeHover", 0 },
                text = "Text",
                icon = "Accent",
                arrow = "Muted",
                desc = "Muted",
            },
            Primary = {
                { "Accent", 0.12, "Accent", 0.4 },
                { "Accent", 0, "Accent", 0 },
                { "Accent", 0.08, "Accent", 0.2 },
                text = "AccentDark",
                icon = "AccentDark",
                arrow = "AccentDark",
                desc = "AccentDark",
                descT = 0.3,
            },
            Danger = {
                { "Error", 0.88, "Error", 0.6 },
                { "Error", 0.78, "Error", 0.25 },
                { "Error", 0.68, "Error", 0.1 },
                text = "Error",
                icon = "Error",
                arrow = "Error",
                desc = "Muted",
            },
        },
    }
    k._btnSpec.card = {
        Default = {
            { "Surface2", 0, "Stroke", 0 },
            { "Surface2", 0, "StrokeHover", 0 },
            { "Surface3", 0.3, "StrokeHover", 0 },
            text = "Text",
            icon = "Accent",
            arrow = "Muted",
            desc = "Muted",
        },
        Primary = k._btnSpec.chip.Primary,
        Danger = k._btnSpec.chip.Danger,
    }
    function k._btnNorm(v)
        v = type(v) == "string" and v:lower() or ""
        if v == "primary" then
            return "Primary"
        end
        if v == "danger" or v == "destructive" then
            return "Danger"
        end
        return "Default"
    end
    
    function k._btnFx(c)
        local hit, bg, stroke, opts = c.hit, c.bg, c.stroke, c.opts
        local spec = k._btnSpec[c.kind or "chip"]
        local texts, descs, icons, arrows = c.texts or {}, c.descs or {}, c.icons or {}, c.arrows or {}
        local state = {
            style = k._btnNorm(opts.Style or (opts.Danger and "Danger") or (opts.Primary and "Primary")),
            hover = false,
            press = false,
            disabled = opts.Disabled == true,
            loading = false,
        }
        local fx = {}
        local function paint(dur)
            local sp = spec[state.style] or spec.Default
            local locked = state.disabled or state.loading
            local idx = 1
            if not locked then
                idx = state.press and 3 or (state.hover and 2 or 1)
            end
            local lv = sp[idx]
            local function fade(t)
                return locked and (1 - (1 - t) * 0.5) or t
            end
            ah(bg, { BackgroundColor3 = z[lv[1]], BackgroundTransparency = fade(lv[2]) }, dur)
            if stroke and not stroke:GetAttribute("Hidden") then
                ah(stroke, { Color = z[lv[3]], Transparency = fade(lv[4]) }, dur)
            end
            local tx = locked and 0.55 or 0
            for _, t in ipairs(texts) do
                ah(t, { TextColor3 = z[sp.text], TextTransparency = tx }, dur)
            end
            for _, t in ipairs(descs) do
                ah(t, { TextColor3 = z[sp.desc], TextTransparency = locked and 0.7 or (sp.descT or 0) }, dur)
            end
            local function tint(list, key)
                for _, im in ipairs(list) do
                    ar(im, z[key], not locked, dur)
                    if not im:GetAttribute("CustomIcon") then
                        ah(im, { ImageTransparency = tx }, dur)
                    end
                end
            end
            tint(icons, sp.icon)
            tint(arrows, sp.arrow)
        end
        local canClick = c.scroller and ay(hit, c.scroller) or function()
            return true
        end
        local last = 0
        local cooldown = tonumber(opts.Cooldown) or 0
        local function run()
            if state.disabled or state.loading then
                return
            end
            if cooldown > 0 then
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - last < cooldown then
                    return
                end
                last = now
            end
            if opts.Lock then
                state.loading = true
                paint(0.12)
                task.spawn(function()
                    aJ(opts.Callback)
                    state.loading = false
                    if hit.Parent then
                        paint(0.2)
                    end
                end)
            else
                aJ(opts.Callback)
            end
        end
        hit.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if ax() then
                return
            end
            state.hover = true
            paint(0.12)
        end)
        hit.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            state.hover, state.press = false, false
            paint(0.25)
        end)
        hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                state.press = true
                paint(0.06)
            end
        end)
        hit.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and state.press then
                state.press = false
                paint(0.12)
            end
        end)
        hit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if state.disabled or state.loading or not canClick() then
                return
            end
            aE(c.ripple or bg)
            run()
        end)
        function fx:SetStyle(s)
            state.style = k._btnNorm(s)
            paint(0.15)
        end
        function fx:SetDisabled(v)
            state.disabled = v == true
            if state.disabled then
                state.hover, state.press = false, false
            end
            paint(0.15)
        end
        function fx:IsDisabled()
            return state.disabled
        end
        function fx:SetCallback(f)
            opts.Callback = f
        end
        function fx:Fire()
            run()
        end
        paint(0)
        return fx
    end
    local function aT(aU, aV)
        local aX = E and 34 or 28
        local aY = aX + 6 + (aV.Desc and 16 or 0)
        local aZ = aO(aU, "TextButton", aY, aV)
        local a_ = ai("Frame", {
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 3),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, aX),
            BackgroundColor3 = z.Surface3,
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.45,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            ClipsDescendants = true,
            Parent = aZ,
        })
        aj(a_, UDim.new(0, 6))
        local a0 = ak(a_)
        local a1 = ai("Frame", {
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Parent = a_,
        })
        ai("UIListLayout", {
            FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
            VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center,
            SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
            Padding = UDim.new(0, 6),
            Parent = a1,
        })
        local icons = {}
        if aV.Icon then
            local img = ai("ImageLabel", {
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
                LayoutOrder = 1,
                Parent = a1,
            })
            aq(img, aV.Icon)
            icons[1] = img
        end
        local title = am({
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
            Text = aV.Name or "Button",
            TextSize = 13,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None,
            LayoutOrder = 2,
            Parent = a1,
        })
        local desc
        if aV.Desc then
            desc = am({
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, aX + 6),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 14),
                Text = aV.Desc,
                TextSize = 12,
                FontFace = D.Regular,
                TextColor3 = z.Muted,
                Parent = aZ,
            })
        end
        local fx = k._btnFx({
            kind = "chip",
            hit = aZ,
            bg = a_,
            stroke = a0,
            opts = aV,
            ripple = a_,
            texts = { title },
            icons = icons,
            scroller = function()
                return aZ:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("ScrollingFrame")
            end,
        })
        local api = {
            SetText = function(_, t)
                title.Text = tostring(t)
            end,
            SetDesc = function(_, t)
                if desc then
                    desc.Text = tostring(t)
                end
            end,
        }
        for name, fn in pairs(fx) do
            api[name] = fn
        end
        return m(aU, aV, api, aZ, "Button")
    end
    function aS:Button(aU)
        aU = l(aU, { Title = "Name", Description = "Desc" })
        if aM(self).Compact then
            return aT(self, aU)
        end
        local aX, aY = aO(self, "TextButton", aU.Desc and J or I, aU)
        aX.ClipsDescendants = true
        local pad, icons = 0, {}
        if aU.Icon then
            local _, img = aB(aX, aU.Icon, z.Accent, UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, 0))
            icons[1] = img
            pad = 26
        end
        local title, desc = aP(aX, aU.Name or "Button", aU.Desc, 30)
        if pad > 0 then
            for _, lbl in ipairs({ title, desc }) do
                lbl.Position = lbl.Position + UDim2.fromOffset(pad, 0)
                lbl.Size = lbl.Size - UDim2.fromOffset(pad, 0)
            end
        end
        local _, arrow = aB(aX, x, z.Muted, UDim2.new(1, -30, 0.5, 0))
        local fx = k._btnFx({
            kind = "card",
            hit = aX,
            bg = aX,
            stroke = aY,
            opts = aU,
            texts = { title },
            descs = desc and { desc } or {},
            icons = icons,
            arrows = { arrow },
            scroller = function()
                return aX:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("ScrollingFrame")
            end,
        })
        local api = {
            SetText = function(_, t)
                title.Text = tostring(t)
            end,
            SetDesc = function(_, t)
                if desc then
                    desc.Text = tostring(t)
                end
            end,
        }
        for name, fn in pairs(fx) do
            api[name] = fn
        end
        return m(self, aU, api, aX, "Button")
    end

        function aS:Toggle(opts)
        local trackW, trackH = E and 44 or 36, E and 24 or 20
        local knobS = E and 18 or 14
        local pad = (trackH - knobS) / 2
        local grow = E and 5 or 4
        local card, stroke, _rowH, titleLbl, descLbl
        opts, card, stroke, _rowH, titleLbl, descLbl = aQ(
            self,
            opts,
            { Title = "Name", Description = "Desc", CurrentValue = "Default", Value = "Default" },
            "TextButton",
            trackW + 22,
            "Toggle"
        )
        local track = ai("Frame", {
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(trackW, trackH),
            BackgroundColor3 = z.Surface3,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Parent = card,
        })
        aj(track, UDim.new(1, 0))
        local trackStroke = ak(track, z.Stroke)
        local knob = ai("Frame", {
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, pad, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(knobS, knobS),
            BackgroundColor3 = z.Muted,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Parent = track,
        })
        aj(knob, UDim.new(1, 0))
        local glow = ai("ImageLabel", {
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 24, 1, 24),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Image = A.Glow,
            ImageColor3 = z.Accent,
            ImageTransparency = 1,
            ZIndex = 0,
            Parent = track,
        })

        local state = { on = opts.Default == true, hover = false, press = false, disabled = opts.Disabled == true }
        local api = { Value = state.on }
        local pulseId = 0

        local function paint(dur, pulse)
            local on, locked = state.on, state.disabled
            local w = knobS + (((state.press and not locked) or pulse) and grow or 0)
            local x = on and (trackW - pad - w) or pad
            ah(track, {
                BackgroundColor3 = on and z.Accent or z.Surface3,
                BackgroundTransparency = locked and 0.5 or 0,
            }, dur)
            ah(trackStroke, {
                Color = on and z.Accent or ((state.hover and not locked) and z.StrokeHover or z.Stroke),
                Transparency = locked and 0.5 or 0,
            }, dur)
            ah(glow, { ImageTransparency = (on and not locked) and 0.75 or 1 }, dur)
            ah(knob, {
                Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0.5, 0),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, knobS),
                BackgroundColor3 = on and z.AccentDark or z.Muted,
                BackgroundTransparency = locked and 0.35 or 0,
            }, dur, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
            ah(titleLbl, { TextTransparency = locked and 0.55 or 0 }, dur)
            if descLbl then
                ah(descLbl, { TextTransparency = locked and 0.7 or 0 }, dur)
            end
        end

        local function setValue(value, silent, byUser)
            value = value == true
            if value == state.on then
                return
            end
            state.on = value
            api.Value = value
            pulseId = pulseId + 1
            local id = pulseId
            if state.press then
                paint(0.2)
            else
                paint(0.1, true)
                task.delay(0.1, function()
                    if id == pulseId then
                        paint(0.25)
                    end
                end)
            end
            if not byUser then
                aF(stroke)
            end
            if not silent then
                aJ(opts.Callback, value)
            end
        end

        card.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if ax() then
                return
            end
            state.hover = true
            paint(0.12)
        end)
        card.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            state.hover, state.press = false, false
            paint(0.2)
        end)
        card.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if aw(input) and not state.disabled then
                state.press = true
                paint(0.08)
            end
        end)
        card.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if aw(input) and state.press then
                state.press = false
                paint(0.15)
            end
        end)
        
        local canTap = ay(card, function()
            return card:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("ScrollingFrame")
        end)
        card.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if state.disabled or not canTap() then
                return
            end
            setValue(not state.on, false, true)
        end)

        function api:Set(value, silent)
            setValue(value, silent, false)
        end
        function api:Get()
            return state.on
        end
        function api:SetDisabled(v)
            state.disabled = v == true
            if state.disabled then
                state.hover, state.press = false, false
            end
            paint(0.15)
        end
        function api:IsDisabled()
            return state.disabled
        end
        function api:SetText(text)
            text = tostring(text)
            titleLbl.Text = text
            api._searchName = text
            api._searchKey = string.lower(text)
        end
        function api:SetDesc(text)
            if descLbl then
                descLbl.Text = tostring(text)
            end
        end

        paint(0)
        local control = m(self, opts, api, card, "Toggle")
        if state.on then
            aJ(opts.Callback, true)
        end
        return control
    end

    function aS:Slider(opts)
        opts = l(opts, {
            Title = "Name",
            Description = "Desc",
            CurrentValue = "Default",
            Value = "Default",
            Increment = "Step",
        })
        local minV = tonumber(opts.Min) or 0
        local maxV = tonumber(opts.Max) or 100
        if minV > maxV then
            minV, maxV = maxV, minV
        end
        local step = tonumber(opts.Step) or 1
        if not (step > 0) then
            step = 1
        end
        local suffix = opts.Suffix ~= nil and tostring(opts.Suffix) or ""

        local decimals = 0
        local function decimalsOf(n)
            local s = string.format("%.8f", math.abs(n)):gsub("0+$", "")
            local dot = s:find(".", 1, true)
            return dot and (#s - dot) or 0
        end
        local function calcDecimals()
            decimals = math.min(math.max(decimalsOf(step), decimalsOf(minV)), 8)
        end
        calcDecimals()
        local function round(v)
            local mult = 10 ^ decimals
            v = math.floor(v * mult + 0.5) / mult
            return v == 0 and 0 or v
        end
        local function snap(v)
            v = math.clamp(v, minV, maxV)
            local snapped = minV + math.floor((v - minV) / step + 0.5) * step
            if snapped > maxV or math.abs(maxV - v) < math.abs(snapped - v) then
                snapped = maxV
            end
            return math.clamp(round(snapped), minV, maxV)
        end
        local function fmt(v)
            return string.format("%." .. decimals .. "f", v)
        end
        local function fractionOf(v)
            local range = maxV - minV
            return range == 0 and 0 or math.clamp((v - minV) / range, 0, 1)
        end
        
        local function parseInput(text)
            text = tostring(text):gsub(",", "."):gsub("%s", "")
            return tonumber(text:match("^[%+%-]?%d*%.?%d*"))
        end

        local metrics = aM(self)
        local compact = metrics.Compact
        local rowH = metrics.Slider
        local card, cardStroke = aO(self, "Frame", rowH, opts)
        aG(card, cardStroke)
        local titleLbl = am({
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, compact and 5 or 12),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -120, 0, 18),
            Text = opts.Name or "Slider",
            TextSize = metrics.TextSize,
            Parent = card,
        })
        local chip = ai("Frame", {
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, compact and -14 or -10, 0, compact and 2 or 9),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 24),
            BackgroundColor3 = z.Surface,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            ClipsDescendants = true,
            Parent = card,
        })
        aj(chip, UDim.new(0, 6))
        local chipStroke = ak(chip)
        local valueLbl = am({
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
            TextSize = 13,
            TextColor3 = z.Accent,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None,
            Parent = chip,
        })
        local editBox = ai("TextBox", {
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = "",
            TextColor3 = z.Text,
            TextSize = 13,
            FontFace = D.Medium,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            ClearTextOnFocus = false,
            Visible = false,
            Parent = chip,
        })
        ai("UISizeConstraint", { MinSize = Vector2.new(14, 0), Parent = editBox })
        local suffixLbl = am({
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
            Text = suffix,
            TextSize = 13,
            TextColor3 = z.Accent,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None,
            Visible = false,
            Parent = chip,
        })
        local chipBtn = ai("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = "",
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            ZIndex = 2,
            Parent = chip,
        })

        local barY = rowH - (compact and 13 or 18)
        local bar = ai("Frame", {
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, barY),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 5),
            BackgroundColor3 = z.Surface3,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Parent = card,
        })
        aj(bar, UDim.new(1, 0))
        local fill = ai("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
            BackgroundColor3 = z.Accent,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Parent = bar,
        })
        aj(fill, UDim.new(1, 0))
        local glow = ai("ImageLabel", {
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Image = A.Glow,
            ImageColor3 = z.Accent,
            ImageTransparency = 0.85,
            ZIndex = 2,
            Parent = bar,
        })
        local thumb = ai("Frame", {
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12),
            BackgroundColor3 = z.Accent,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            ZIndex = 3,
            Parent = bar,
        })
        aj(thumb, UDim.new(1, 0))
        
        local hitTop = math.max((compact and 2 or 9) + 24, math.floor(barY + 2.5 - (E and 20 or 14)))
        local hit = ai("TextButton", {
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, hitTop),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, rowH - hitTop),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = "",
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            ZIndex = 4,
            Parent = card,
        })

        local state = { dragging = false, hover = false, chipHover = false, editing = false, disabled = opts.Disabled == true }
        local api = { Value = 0, Dragging = false }
        api.Value = snap(tonumber(opts.Default) or minV)
        local pulseId = 0
        local dragInput, lockedScroller

        local function paintChip(dur)
            local color, alpha = z.Stroke, 0
            if state.editing then
                color = z.StrokeHover
            elseif state.dragging then
                color, alpha = z.Accent, 0.55
            elseif state.chipHover and not state.disabled then
                color = z.StrokeHover
            end
            ah(chipStroke, { Color = color, Transparency = alpha }, dur)
        end
        local function paintThumb(dur)
            local size, glowSize, glowAlpha = 12, 28, 0.85
            if state.disabled then
                glowAlpha = 1
            elseif state.dragging then
                size, glowSize, glowAlpha = 16, 44, 0.7
            elseif state.hover then
                glowSize, glowAlpha = 34, 0.78
            end
            ah(
                thumb,
                { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size) },
                dur,
                state.dragging and Enum.EasingStyle.Back or Enum.EasingStyle.Quint
            )
            ah(glow, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(glowSize, glowSize), ImageTransparency = glowAlpha }, dur, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
        end
        local function paintDisabled(dur)
            local locked = state.disabled
            ah(titleLbl, { TextTransparency = locked and 0.55 or 0 }, dur)
            ah(valueLbl, { TextTransparency = locked and 0.55 or 0 }, dur)
            ah(bar, { BackgroundTransparency = locked and 0.5 or 0 }, dur)
            ah(fill, { BackgroundTransparency = locked and 0.6 or 0 }, dur)
            ah(thumb, { BackgroundTransparency = locked and 0.4 or 0 }, dur)
        end
        local function paintAll(dur)
            paintDisabled(dur)
            paintThumb(dur)
            paintChip(dur)
        end

        local function resizeChip(instant)
            local width
            if state.editing then
                local boxW = editBox.AbsoluteSize.X / self.Window.Scale.Scale
                width = 9 + boxW + suffixLbl.TextBounds.X + 9
                suffixLbl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(9 + boxW, 0)
            else
                width = valueLbl.TextBounds.X + 18
            end
            width = math.max(width, 36)
            if instant then
                chip.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, 24)
            else
                ah(chip, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, 24) }, 0.2)
            end
        end
        valueLbl:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextBounds"):Connect(function()
            if not state.editing then
                resizeChip(false)
            end
        end)
        editBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
            if state.editing then
                resizeChip(false)
            end
        end)

        local function place(frac, dur, style)
            local pos = UDim2.new(frac, 0, 0.5, 0)
            ah(fill, { Size = UDim2.new(frac, 0, 1, 0) }, dur, style)
            ah(thumb, { Position = pos }, dur, style)
            ah(glow, { Position = pos }, dur, style)
        end
        local function applyVisual(dur, style)
            place(fractionOf(api.Value), dur, style)
            valueLbl.Text = fmt(api.Value) .. suffix
        end
        local function pulse()
            pulseId = pulseId + 1
            local id = pulseId
            ah(thumb, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 12) }, 0.08)
            ah(glow, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 32), ImageTransparency = 0.78 }, 0.12)
            task.delay(0.1, function()
                if id == pulseId and not state.dragging then
                    paintThumb(0.25)
                end
            end)
        end

        function api:Set(value, silent)
            value = tonumber(value)
            if not value or value ~= value then
                return
            end
            value = snap(value)
            if value == api.Value then
                return
            end
            api.Value = value
            if state.dragging then
                applyVisual(0.07, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
            else
                applyVisual(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
                pulse()
                aF(cardStroke)
            end
            if not silent then
                aJ(opts.Callback, value)
            end
        end
        function api:Get()
            return api.Value
        end

        local function valueFromX(x)
            local frac = math.clamp((x - bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(bar.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1)
            api:Set(minV + (maxV - minV) * frac)
        end
        local function unlockScroll()
            if lockedScroller then
                lockedScroller.ScrollingEnabled = true
                lockedScroller = nil
            end
        end
        local function beginDrag(input)
            if state.dragging or state.disabled then
                return
            end
            state.dragging = true
            api.Dragging = true
            dragInput = input
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                
                local scroller = card:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("ScrollingFrame")
                if scroller and scroller.ScrollingEnabled then
                    scroller.ScrollingEnabled = false
                    lockedScroller = scroller
                end
            end
            paintThumb(0.15)
            paintChip(0.12)
            valueFromX(input.Position.X)
        end
        local function endDrag()
            if not state.dragging then
                return
            end
            state.dragging = false
            api.Dragging = false
            dragInput = nil
            unlockScroll()
            paintThumb(0.2)
            paintChip(0.2)
            applyVisual(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
            aJ(opts.OnRelease, api.Value)
        end

        hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if aw(input) then
                beginDrag(input)
            end
        end)
        hit.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if ax() then
                return
            end
            state.hover = true
            paintThumb(0.15)
        end)
        hit.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            state.hover = false
            paintThumb(0.2)
        end)
        self.Window:_listen("Changed", function(input)
            if not (state.dragging and dragInput) then
                return
            end
            if dragInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                if input == dragInput then
                    valueFromX(input.Position.X)
                end
            elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
                
                local ok, pressed = pcall(b.IsMouseButtonPressed, b, Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
                if ok and not pressed then
                    endDrag()
                else
                    valueFromX(input.Position.X)
                end
            end
        end, api)
        self.Window:_listen("Ended", function(input)
            if not (state.dragging and dragInput) then
                return
            end
            if input == dragInput
                or (dragInput.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
            then
                endDrag()
            end
        end, api)
        
        api._listeners = api._listeners or {}
        table.insert(api._listeners, unlockScroll)

        chipBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if ax() then
                return
            end
            state.chipHover = true
            paintChip(0.12)
        end)
        chipBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            state.chipHover = false
            paintChip(0.2)
        end)
        chipBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if state.disabled then
                return
            end
            state.editing = true
            state.chipHover = false
            editBox.Text = fmt(api.Value)
            valueLbl.Visible = false
            editBox.Visible = true
            suffixLbl.Visible = suffix ~= ""
            chipBtn.Visible = false
            paintChip(0.12)
            editBox:CaptureFocus()
            task.defer(resizeChip, false)
        end)
        editBox.Focused:Connect(function()
            
            task.defer(function()
                if editBox:IsFocused() then
                    editBox.SelectionStart = 1
                    editBox.CursorPosition = #editBox.Text + 1
                end
            end)
        end)
        editBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(_enter, inputObject)
            local cancelled = inputObject ~= nil and inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape
            local parsed = (not cancelled) and parseInput(editBox.Text) or nil
            state.editing = false
            state.chipHover = false
            editBox.Visible = false
            suffixLbl.Visible = false
            valueLbl.Visible = true
            chipBtn.Visible = true
            paintChip(0.2)
            if parsed then
                api:Set(parsed)
            end
            resizeChip(false)
        end)

        function api:SetDisabled(v)
            state.disabled = v == true
            if state.disabled then
                if state.dragging then
                    endDrag()
                end
                if state.editing then
                    editBox:ReleaseFocus()
                end
                state.hover, state.chipHover = false, false
            end
            paintAll(0.15)
        end
        function api:IsDisabled()
            return state.disabled
        end
        function api:SetText(text)
            text = tostring(text)
            titleLbl.Text = text
            api._searchName = text
            api._searchKey = string.lower(text)
        end
        function api:SetSuffix(text)
            suffix = text ~= nil and tostring(text) or ""
            suffixLbl.Text = suffix
            applyVisual(0, nil)
            resizeChip(false)
        end
        
        function api:SetRange(newMin, newMax, newStep, silent)
            newMin = tonumber(newMin) or minV
            newMax = tonumber(newMax) or maxV
            if newMin > newMax then
                newMin, newMax = newMax, newMin
            end
            minV, maxV = newMin, newMax
            newStep = tonumber(newStep)
            if newStep and newStep > 0 then
                step = newStep
            end
            calcDecimals()
            local clamped = snap(api.Value)
            local changed = clamped ~= api.Value
            api.Value = clamped
            applyVisual(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
            if changed and not silent then
                aJ(opts.Callback, clamped)
            end
        end

        applyVisual(0, nil)
        paintAll(0)
        task.defer(resizeChip, true)
        return m(self, opts, api, card, "Slider")
    end

    function aS:Dropdown(opt)
        opt = l(opt, {
            Title = "Name",
            Description = "Desc",
            CurrentOption = "Default",
            Value = "Default",
            MultipleOptions = "Multi",
            Values = "Options",
        })
        if opt.Multi and type(opt.Default) ~= "table" and opt.Default ~= nil then
            opt.Default = { opt.Default }
        elseif not opt.Multi and type(opt.Default) == "table" then
            opt.Default = opt.Default[1]
        end
        local isMulti = opt.Multi == true

        local foldMap = k._foldMap
        if not foldMap then
            foldMap = {}
            for _, group in ipairs({
                { "a", "\u{e0}\u{e1}\u{1ea3}\u{e3}\u{1ea1}\u{103}\u{1eb1}\u{1eaf}\u{1eb3}\u{1eb5}\u{1eb7}\u{e2}\u{1ea7}\u{1ea5}\u{1ea9}\u{1eab}\u{1ead}\u{e4}\u{e5}", "\u{c0}\u{c1}\u{1ea2}\u{c3}\u{1ea0}\u{102}\u{1eb0}\u{1eae}\u{1eb2}\u{1eb4}\u{1eb6}\u{c2}\u{1ea6}\u{1ea4}\u{1ea8}\u{1eaa}\u{1eac}\u{c4}\u{c5}" },
                { "e", "\u{e8}\u{e9}\u{1ebb}\u{1ebd}\u{1eb9}\u{ea}\u{1ec1}\u{1ebf}\u{1ec3}\u{1ec5}\u{1ec7}\u{eb}", "\u{c8}\u{c9}\u{1eba}\u{1ebc}\u{1eb8}\u{ca}\u{1ec0}\u{1ebe}\u{1ec2}\u{1ec4}\u{1ec6}\u{cb}" },
                { "i", "\u{ec}\u{ed}\u{1ec9}\u{129}\u{1ecb}\u{ef}", "\u{cc}\u{cd}\u{1ec8}\u{128}\u{1eca}\u{cf}" },
                { "o", "\u{f2}\u{f3}\u{1ecf}\u{f5}\u{1ecd}\u{f4}\u{1ed3}\u{1ed1}\u{1ed5}\u{1ed7}\u{1ed9}\u{1a1}\u{1edd}\u{1edb}\u{1edf}\u{1ee1}\u{1ee3}\u{f6}", "\u{d2}\u{d3}\u{1ece}\u{d5}\u{1ecc}\u{d4}\u{1ed2}\u{1ed0}\u{1ed4}\u{1ed6}\u{1ed8}\u{1a0}\u{1edc}\u{1eda}\u{1ede}\u{1ee0}\u{1ee2}\u{d6}" },
                { "u", "\u{f9}\u{fa}\u{1ee7}\u{169}\u{1ee5}\u{1b0}\u{1eeb}\u{1ee9}\u{1eed}\u{1eef}\u{1ef1}\u{fc}", "\u{d9}\u{da}\u{1ee6}\u{168}\u{1ee4}\u{1af}\u{1eea}\u{1ee8}\u{1eec}\u{1eee}\u{1ef0}\u{dc}" },
                { "y", "\u{1ef3}\u{fd}\u{1ef7}\u{1ef9}\u{1ef5}\u{ff}", "\u{1ef2}\u{dd}\u{1ef6}\u{1ef8}\u{1ef4}\u{178}" },
                { "d", "\u{111}", "\u{110}" },
                { "n", "\u{f1}", "\u{d1}" },
                { "c", "\u{e7}", "\u{c7}" },
            }) do
                for index = 2, #group do
                    for ch in string.gmatch(group[index], utf8.charpattern) do
                        foldMap[ch] = group[1]
                    end
                end
            end
            k._foldMap = foldMap
        end
        local function foldText(text)
            text = string.lower(text)
            if string.find(text, "[\128-\255]") then
                text = string.gsub(text, utf8.charpattern, foldMap)
            end
            return text
        end
        local function escapeRich(text)
            return (text:gsub("&", "&amp;"):gsub("<", "&lt;"):gsub(">", "&gt;"))
        end
        local function utf8Length(text)
            return utf8.len(text) or #text
        end
        local function utf8Prefix(text, count)
            if count <= 0 then
                return ""
            end
            local ok, position = pcall(utf8.offset, text, count + 1)
            if ok and position then
                return string.sub(text, 1, position - 1)
            end
            return string.sub(text, 1, count)
        end
        
        local function foldChars(text)
            local parts, byteChar, charStart, charEnd = {}, {}, {}, {}
            local count, bytes = 0, 0
            for position, ch in string.gmatch(text, "()(" .. utf8.charpattern .. ")") do
                count += 1
                charStart[count] = position
                charEnd[count] = position + #ch - 1
                local folded = foldMap[ch] or string.lower(ch)
                parts[count] = folded
                for _ = 1, #folded do
                    bytes += 1
                    byteChar[bytes] = count
                end
            end
            return table.concat(parts), byteChar, charStart, charEnd
        end

        local options, foldedOf = {}, {}
        local function setOptions(list)
            options, foldedOf = {}, {}
            for _, option in ipairs(type(list) == "table" and list or {}) do
                if option ~= nil and foldedOf[option] == nil then
                    options[#options + 1] = option
                    foldedOf[option] = foldText(tostring(option))
                end
            end
        end
        setOptions(opt.Options)

        local frame, frameStroke, rowHeight
        opt, frame, frameStroke, rowHeight = aQ(self, opt, {}, "Frame")
        local metrics = aM(self)
        local compact = metrics.Compact
        local chipH = metrics.Chip
        local window = self.Window
        local itemH = E and 40 or 30
        local maxRows = math.max(math.floor(tonumber(opt.MaxRows) or 6), 1)
        local stacked = compact and opt.Stacked == true
        local topPad = 2
        if stacked then
            topPad = (opt.Name or "") ~= "" and 20 or 2
            chipH = E and 34 or 30
            rowHeight = topPad + chipH + 4
            frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, rowHeight)
        end
        local button = ai("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, rowHeight),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = "",
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            Parent = frame,
        })
        local titleLabel, descLabel
        if not stacked then
            titleLabel, descLabel = aP(button, opt.Name or "Dropdown", opt.Desc, 180, metrics)
        elseif topPad > 2 then
            titleLabel = am({
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 1),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 16),
                Text = opt.Name,
                TextSize = metrics.TextSize,
                Parent = button,
            })
        end
        local chip = ai("Frame", {
            AnchorPoint = stacked and Vector2.zero or Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
            Position = stacked and UDim2.fromOffset(14, topPad) or UDim2.new(1, compact and -14 or -10, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, chipH),
            BackgroundColor3 = z.Surface,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            ClipsDescendants = true,
            Parent = button,
        })
        aj(chip, UDim.new(0, 6))
        local chipStroke = ak(chip)
        local valueLabel = am({
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -34, 1, 0),
            TextColor3 = z.Muted,
            TextSize = 13,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None,
            ClipsDescendants = true,
            Parent = chip,
        })
        local arrow = aC(chip, 1, -6)
        local measure = am({
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, chipH),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
            TextSize = 13,
            Visible = false,
            Parent = chip,
        })
        local displayText = ""
        local function fitText()
            local maxWidth = (stacked and frame.AbsoluteSize.X / window.Scale.Scale - 28 or compact and aN(frame, window) or 190) - 10 - 34
            measure.Text = displayText
            if measure.TextBounds.X <= maxWidth then
                return displayText
            end
            
            local low, high, best = 1, utf8Length(displayText) - 1, 0
            while low <= high do
                local mid = math.floor((low + high) / 2)
                measure.Text = utf8Prefix(displayText, mid) .. ".."
                if measure.TextBounds.X <= maxWidth then
                    best = mid
                    low = mid + 1
                else
                    high = mid - 1
                end
            end
            return best > 0 and (utf8Prefix(displayText, best) .. "..") or ".."
        end
        local function resizeChip(instant)
            if stacked then
                chip.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, chipH)
                return
            end
            local width
            if compact then
                width = aN(frame, window)
            else
                width = math.clamp(valueLabel.TextBounds.X + 10 + 34, 60, 190)
            end
            aR(titleLabel, descLabel, width + (compact and 24 or 20))
            if instant then
                chip.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, chipH)
            else
