                ah(chip, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, chipH) }, 0.2)
            end
        end
        valueLabel:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextBounds"):Connect(function()
            if not compact then
                resizeChip(false)
            end
        end)
        if compact then
            local lastWidth, pending = 0, false
            frame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
                local width = frame.AbsoluteSize.X
                if width == lastWidth then
                    return
                end
                lastWidth = width
                resizeChip(true)
                if not pending then
                    pending = true
                    task.delay(0.12, function()
                        pending = false
                        valueLabel.Text = fitText()
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end

        local popup = ai("Frame", {
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(220, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Visible = false,
            ZIndex = 45,
            Parent = window.Body,
        })
        popup:SetAttribute("NoDrag", true)
        local shadow = ai("ImageLabel", {
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-18, -12),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 36, 1, 36),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Image = A.Shadow,
            ImageColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0),
            ImageTransparency = 1,
            ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice,
            SliceCenter = Rect.new(49, 49, 450, 450),
            Parent = popup,
        })
        local content = ai("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
            BackgroundColor3 = z.Background,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            ClipsDescendants = true,
            Parent = popup,
        })
        aj(content, UDim.new(0, 10))
        local popupStroke = ak(content, z.Stroke, 1)
        ao(content)
        local padded = ai("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 120), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = content })
        al(padded, 8, 8, 8, 8)

        local searchBar = ai("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32),
            BackgroundColor3 = z.Surface,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Parent = padded,
        })
        aj(searchBar, UDim.new(0, 7))
        local searchStroke = ak(searchBar)
        local searchIcon, searchIconImage = aB(searchBar, "search", z.Muted, UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, 0))
        searchIcon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14)
        local searchBox = ai("TextBox", {
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = "",
            PlaceholderText = opt.SearchPlaceholder or "Search",
            PlaceholderColor3 = z.Muted,
            TextColor3 = z.Text,
            TextSize = 13,
            FontFace = D.Regular,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
            ClearTextOnFocus = false,
            Parent = searchBar,
        })
        searchBox.Focused:Connect(function()
            ah(searchStroke, { Color = z.StrokeHover }, 0.15)
            ah(searchIconImage, { ImageColor3 = z.Accent }, 0.15)
        end)

        local toolbar = ai("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Visible = isMulti,
            Parent = padded,
        })
        local function makeToolbarButton(text, position)
            local toolButton = ai("TextButton", {
                Position = position,
                Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -3, 1, 0),
                BackgroundColor3 = z.Surface3,
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.35,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Text = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                ClipsDescendants = true,
                Parent = toolbar,
            })
            aj(toolButton, UDim.new(0, 6))
            local toolStroke = ak(toolButton)
            am({
                Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
                Text = text,
                TextSize = 12,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
                Parent = toolButton,
            })
            toolButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                ah(toolButton, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.05 }, 0.15)
                ah(toolStroke, { Color = z.StrokeHover }, 0.15)
            end)
            toolButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                ah(toolButton, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.35 }, 0.25)
                ah(toolStroke, { Color = z.Stroke }, 0.25)
            end)
            toolButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                aE(toolButton)
            end)
            return toolButton
        end
        local selectAllButton = makeToolbarButton(opt.SelectAllText or "Select all", UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0))
        local clearAllButton = makeToolbarButton(opt.ClearAllText or "Clear all", UDim2.new(0.5, 3, 0, 0))

        local list = ai("ScrollingFrame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 100),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            ScrollBarThickness = E and 4 or 3,
            ScrollBarImageColor3 = z.Accent,
            ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.4,
            ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
            CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
            Parent = padded,
        })
        local emptyLabel = am({
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, itemH),
            Text = opt.NoResultsText or "No results",
            TextSize = 13,
            FontFace = D.Regular,
            TextColor3 = z.Muted,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
            Visible = false,
            Parent = list,
        })

        local STEP = itemH + 2 
        local BUFFER = 4 
        local by = { Open = false }
        local selected = {}
        if isMulti then
            for _, value in ipairs(opt.Default or {}) do
                selected[value] = true
            end
        elseif opt.Default ~= nil then
            selected[opt.Default] = true
        end
        local query, tokens = "", {}
        local filtered = options
        local active, activeSource = nil, nil
        local rows, rowAt = {}, {}
        local firstShown, lastShown = nil, nil
        local canvasH, listH = 0, 0
        local popupSize = Vector2.new(220, 120)
        local popupReady = false
        local searchVisible = true
        local parentScroller, lockedScroller = nil, nil
        local renderConn = nil
        local openToken = 0
        local lastActivate = 0
        local setActive, toggleOption, setOpen

        local function getValue()
            if isMulti then
                local out = {}
                for _, option in ipairs(options) do
                    if selected[option] then
                        out[#out + 1] = option
                    end
                end
                return out
            end
            for _, option in ipairs(options) do
                if selected[option] then
                    return option
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function tokenize(text)
            local result = {}
            for word in string.gmatch(foldText(text), "%S+") do
                result[#result + 1] = word
            end
            return result
        end
        local function rebuildFiltered()
            if #tokens == 0 then
                filtered = options
                return
            end
            filtered = {}
            for _, option in ipairs(options) do
                local folded = foldedOf[option]
                local matched = true
                for _, token in ipairs(tokens) do
                    if not string.find(folded, token, 1, true) then
                        matched = false
                        break
                    end
                end
                if matched then
                    filtered[#filtered + 1] = option
                end
            end
        end
        local function labelFor(option)
            local text = tostring(option)
            if #tokens == 0 then
                return escapeRich(text)
            end
            local folded, byteChar, charStart, charEnd
            if string.find(text, "[\128-\255]") then
                folded, byteChar, charStart, charEnd = foldChars(text)
            else
                folded = string.lower(text)
            end
            local ranges = {}
            for _, token in ipairs(tokens) do
                local first, last = string.find(folded, token, 1, true)
                if first then
                    if byteChar then
                        first, last = charStart[byteChar[first]], charEnd[byteChar[last]]
                    end
                    if first and last then
                        ranges[#ranges + 1] = { first, last }
                    end
                end
            end
            if #ranges == 0 then
                return escapeRich(text)
            end
            table.sort(ranges, function(x, y)
                return x[1] < y[1]
            end)
            local merged = {}
            for _, range in ipairs(ranges) do
                local previous = merged[#merged]
                if previous and range[1] <= previous[2] + 1 then
                    if range[2] > previous[2] then
                        previous[2] = range[2]
                    end
                else
                    merged[#merged + 1] = { range[1], range[2] }
                end
            end
            local hex = z.Accent:ToHex()
            local parts, position = {}, 1
            for _, range in ipairs(merged) do
                parts[#parts + 1] = escapeRich(string.sub(text, position, range[1] - 1))
                parts[#parts + 1] = '<font color="#' .. hex .. '"><b>' .. escapeRich(string.sub(text, range[1], range[2])) .. "</b></font>"
                position = range[2] + 1
            end
            parts[#parts + 1] = escapeRich(string.sub(text, position))
            return table.concat(parts)
        end

        local function rowTween(row, instance, props, time, style)
            local tween = ah(instance, props, time, style)
            if tween then
                local pendingTweens = row.Tweens
                pendingTweens[#pendingTweens + 1] = tween
                if #pendingTweens > 8 then
                    table.remove(pendingTweens, 1)
                end
            end
        end
        local function paintHover(row, time)
            local on, hover = row.On == true, row.Hover == true
            rowTween(row, row.Frame, {
                BackgroundColor3 = on and z.Accent or z.Surface3,
                BackgroundTransparency = on and (hover and 0.8 or 0.88) or (hover and 0.55 or 1),
            }, time)
            rowTween(row, row.Label, { TextColor3 = (on or hover) and z.Text or z.Muted }, time)
            if row.BoxStroke and not on then
                rowTween(row, row.BoxStroke, { Color = hover and z.StrokeHover or z.Stroke }, time)
            end
        end
        local function paintSelected(row, animate)
            local on = selected[row.Option] == true
            if row.On == on and animate then
                return
            end
            row.On = on
            local time = animate and 0.18 or 0
            paintHover(row, time)
            if isMulti then
                rowTween(row, row.Box, { BackgroundColor3 = on and z.Accent or z.Surface2 }, time)
                rowTween(row, row.BoxStroke, { Color = on and z.Accent or (row.Hover and z.StrokeHover or z.Stroke) }, time)
            else
                rowTween(row, row.Bar, {
                    BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1,
                    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, on and itemH - 14 or 4),
                }, animate and 0.3 or 0, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
            end
            rowTween(row, row.Check, { ImageTransparency = on and 0 or 1 }, time)
            if animate and on then
                row.CheckScale.Scale = 0.5
                rowTween(row, row.CheckScale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
            else
                row.CheckScale.Scale = 1
            end
        end
        local function refreshDisplay(animated)
            local value = getValue()
            if #options == 0 then
                displayText = opt.EmptyText or "None"
            elseif isMulti then
                if #value > 2 then
                    displayText = tostring(value[1]) .. ", " .. tostring(value[2]) .. "  +" .. (#value - 2)
                else
                    local names = {}
                    for index, option in ipairs(value) do
                        names[index] = tostring(option)
                    end
                    displayText = #names > 0 and table.concat(names, ", ") or (opt.NoneText or "None")
                end
            else
                displayText = value ~= nil and tostring(value) or (opt.NoneText or "None")
            end
            valueLabel.Text = fitText()
            local hasValue = #options > 0 and ((isMulti and #value > 0) or (not isMulti and value ~= nil))
            ah(valueLabel, { TextColor3 = hasValue and z.Text or z.Muted }, animated and 0.15 or 0)
            for _, row in ipairs(rows) do
                if row.Index then
                    paintSelected(row, animated)
                end
            end
        end

        local function makeRow()
            local rowFrame = ai("TextButton", {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, itemH),
                BackgroundColor3 = z.Surface3,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Text = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Visible = false,
                Parent = list,
            })
            aj(rowFrame, UDim.new(0, 7))
            local row = { Frame = rowFrame, Tweens = {}, Hover = false }
            if isMulti then
                row.Box = ai("Frame", {
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
                    Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, 0),
                    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18),
                    BackgroundColor3 = z.Surface2,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    Parent = rowFrame,
                })
                aj(row.Box, UDim.new(0, 5))
                row.BoxStroke = ak(row.Box, z.Stroke)
                row.Check = ai("ImageLabel", {
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
                    Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
                    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    ImageColor3 = z.AccentDark,
                    ImageTransparency = 1,
                    ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
                    Parent = row.Box,
                })
            else
                row.Bar = ai("Frame", {
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
                    Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, 0),
                    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 4),
                    BackgroundColor3 = z.Accent,
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    Parent = rowFrame,
                })
                aj(row.Bar, UDim.new(1, 0))
                row.Check = ai("ImageLabel", {
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
                    Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0),
                    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    ImageColor3 = z.Accent,
                    ImageTransparency = 1,
                    ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit,
                    Parent = rowFrame,
                })
            end
            aq(row.Check, "check")
            row.CheckScale = ai("UIScale", { Parent = row.Check })
            row.Label = am({
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(isMulti and 36 or 14, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, isMulti and -44 or -46, 1, 0),
                Text = "",
                TextSize = 13,
                TextColor3 = z.Muted,
                RichText = true,
                Parent = rowFrame,
            })
            local canClick = ay(rowFrame, function()
                return list
            end)
            rowFrame.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                if ax() or not row.Index then
                    return
                end
                setActive(row.Index, "mouse", false)
            end)
            rowFrame.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                if activeSource == "mouse" and row.Index and active == row.Index then
                    setActive(nil, "mouse", false)
                end
            end)
            rowFrame.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if not canClick() or not row.Index then
                    return
                end
                toggleOption(row.Option)
            end)
            rows[#rows + 1] = row
            return row
        end
        local function bindRow(row, index, option)
            for _, tween in ipairs(row.Tweens) do
                tween:Cancel()
            end
            table.clear(row.Tweens)
            row.Index, row.Option = index, option
            row.Hover = active == index
            row.On = nil
            row.Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, (index - 1) * STEP)
            row.Label.Text = labelFor(option)
            row.Frame.Visible = true
            paintSelected(row, false)
        end
        local function renderRows(force)
            if not by.Open then
                return
            end
            local total = #filtered
            local top = list.CanvasPosition.Y
            local first = math.max(1, math.floor(top / STEP) + 1 - BUFFER)
            local last = math.min(total, math.ceil((top + listH) / STEP) + BUFFER)
            if not force and first == firstShown and last == lastShown then
                return
            end
            firstShown, lastShown = first, last
            table.clear(rowAt)
            local free = {}
            for _, row in ipairs(rows) do
                local index = row.Index
                if index and index >= first and index <= last then
                    rowAt[index] = row
                else
                    row.Index = nil
                    row.Frame.Visible = false
                    free[#free + 1] = row
                end
            end
            for index = first, last do
                if not rowAt[index] then
                    local row = table.remove(free) or makeRow()
                    bindRow(row, index, filtered[index])
                    rowAt[index] = row
                end
            end
        end
        local function invalidateRows()
            for _, row in ipairs(rows) do
                row.Index = nil
                row.Frame.Visible = false
            end
            table.clear(rowAt)
            firstShown, lastShown = nil, nil
        end
        list:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanvasPosition"):Connect(function()
            renderRows(false)
        end)
        local function scrollToIndex(index)
            local top = (index - 1) * STEP
            local bottom = top + itemH
            local current = list.CanvasPosition.Y
            local target = current
            if top < current then
                target = top
            elseif bottom > current + listH then
                target = bottom - listH
            end
            if target ~= current then
                list.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.clamp(target, 0, math.max(0, canvasH - listH)))
            end
            renderRows(false)
        end
        local function scrollToSelected()
            local target = 0
            if not isMulti then
                local value = getValue()
                if value ~= nil then
                    for index, option in ipairs(filtered) do
                        if option == value then
                            target = math.max(0, (index - 2) * STEP)
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            list.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.clamp(target, 0, math.max(0, canvasH - listH)))
        end
        setActive = function(index, source, scroll)
            activeSource = source
            if active == index then
                return
            end
            local previous = active
            active = index
            local previousRow = previous and rowAt[previous]
            if previousRow then
                previousRow.Hover = false
                paintHover(previousRow, 0.15)
            end
            local currentRow = index and rowAt[index]
            if currentRow then
                currentRow.Hover = true
                paintHover(currentRow, 0.15)
            end
            if scroll and index then
                scrollToIndex(index)
            end
        end

        local function layoutPopup(instant)
            searchVisible = opt.Searchable ~= false and #options > (opt.SearchAfter or 0)
            searchBar.Visible = searchVisible
            local topH = 0
            if searchVisible then
                topH += 38
            end
            toolbar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, topH)
            if isMulti and #options > 0 then
                toolbar.Visible = true
                topH += 34
            else
                toolbar.Visible = false
            end
            local count = #filtered
            emptyLabel.Visible = count == 0
            local shown = math.clamp(count, 1, maxRows)
            local height = shown * itemH + (shown - 1) * 2
            canvasH = math.max(count, 1) * STEP - 2
            list.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, canvasH)
            local scale = window.Scale.Scale
            local host = window:_popupHost(frame)
            local hostHeight = host.AbsoluteSize.Y / scale
            local chipY = (chip.AbsolutePosition.Y - host.AbsolutePosition.Y) / scale
            local room = math.max(hostHeight - (chipY + chip.AbsoluteSize.Y / scale) - 14, chipY - 14) - topH - 16
            if room >= itemH then
                height = math.min(height, room)
            end
            listH = height
            list.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, topH)
            list.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height)
            local width = math.max(math.floor(chip.AbsoluteSize.X / scale), opt.PopupWidth or 210)
            width = math.min(width, math.max(host.AbsoluteSize.X / scale - 16, 120))
            popupSize = Vector2.new(width, topH + height + 16)
            padded.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, popupSize.Y)
            if not popupReady then
                return
            end
            if instant then
                popup.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(popupSize.X, popupSize.Y)
            else
                ah(popup, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(popupSize.X, popupSize.Y) }, 0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
            end
        end
        local function alignPopup()
            local host = window:_popupHost(frame)
            local scale = window.Scale.Scale
            if parentScroller then
                local center = chip.AbsolutePosition + chip.AbsoluteSize / 2
                if not az(center, parentScroller) then
                    by:SetOpen(false)
                    return
                end
            end
            local hostSize = host.AbsoluteSize / scale
            local chipPos = (chip.AbsolutePosition - host.AbsolutePosition) / scale
            local chipSize = chip.AbsoluteSize / scale
            local x = math.clamp(chipPos.X + chipSize.X, popupSize.X + 8, math.max(hostSize.X - 8, popupSize.X + 8))
            local y = chipPos.Y + chipSize.Y + 6
            if y + popupSize.Y > hostSize.Y - 8 and chipPos.Y - 6 - popupSize.Y >= 8 then
                popup.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
                popup.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, chipPos.Y - 6)
                content.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
                content.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1)
            else
                popup.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
                popup.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
                content.AnchorPoint = Vector2.zero
                content.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0)
            end
        end
        local function unlockScroll()
            if lockedScroller then
                lockedScroller.ScrollingEnabled = true
                lockedScroller = nil
            end
        end

        local function commit()
            refreshDisplay(true)
            aJ(opt.Callback, getValue())
        end
        toggleOption = function(option)
            if selected[option] then
                if not isMulti and opt.AllowNone == false then
                    setOpen(false)
                    return
                end
                selected[option] = nil
            else
                if isMulti then
                    local limit = tonumber(opt.MaxSelected)
                    if limit and #getValue() >= limit then
                        ah(popupStroke, { Color = z.Warning }, 0.08)
                        task.delay(0.3, function()
                            ah(popupStroke, { Color = z.Stroke }, 0.3)
                        end)
                        return
                    end
                else
                    selected = {}
                end
                selected[option] = true
            end
            commit()
            if not isMulti and opt.CloseOnSelect ~= false then
                setOpen(false)
            end
        end
        setOpen = function(state)
            if state == by.Open then
                return
            end
            by.Open = state
            arrow:Set(state)
            ah(chipStroke, { Color = state and z.Accent or z.Stroke, Transparency = state and 0.35 or 0 }, 0.2)
            ah(chip, { BackgroundColor3 = state and z.Surface2 or z.Surface }, 0.2)
            openToken += 1
            if state then
                if window._closePopup and window._closePopup ~= by._close then
                    window._closePopup()
                end
                window._closePopup = by._close
                parentScroller = frame:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("ScrollingFrame")
                if parentScroller and parentScroller.ScrollingEnabled then
                    lockedScroller = parentScroller
                    parentScroller.ScrollingEnabled = false
                end
                popup.Parent = window:_popupHost(frame)
                query, tokens = "", {}
                searchBox.Text = ""
                searchBox.PlaceholderText = opt.SearchPlaceholder or (#options > 6 and ("Search " .. #options .. " options") or "Search")
                active, activeSource = nil, nil
                rebuildFiltered()
                invalidateRows()
                layoutPopup(true)
                refreshDisplay(false)
                scrollToSelected()
                popupReady = true
                popup.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(popupSize.X, 0)
                alignPopup()
                popup.Visible = true
                renderRows(true)
                ah(popup, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(popupSize.X, popupSize.Y) }, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
                ah(popupStroke, { Transparency = 0 }, 0.12)
                ah(shadow, { ImageTransparency = 0.4 }, 0.3)
                if not renderConn then
                    renderConn = window:_listen("Render", alignPopup)
                end
                if searchVisible and not E and opt.AutoFocus ~= false then
                    local token = openToken
                    task.defer(function()
                        if by.Open and openToken == token then
                            pcall(function()
                                searchBox:CaptureFocus()
                            end)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            else
                if window._closePopup == by._close then
                    window._closePopup = nil
                end
                unlockScroll()
                if searchBox:IsFocused() then
                    searchBox:ReleaseFocus()
                end
                popupReady = false
                active, activeSource = nil, nil
                ah(popup, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(popupSize.X, 0) }, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
                ah(shadow, { ImageTransparency = 1 }, 0.16)
                local token = openToken
                task.delay(0.14, function()
                    if token == openToken and not by.Open then
                        ah(popupStroke, { Transparency = 1 }, 0.06)
                    end
                end)
                task.delay(0.21, function()
                    if token == openToken and not by.Open then
                        popup.Visible = false
                        if renderConn then
                            renderConn()
                            renderConn = nil
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        by._close = function()
            setOpen(false)
        end

        local function firstSelectedIndex()
            for index, option in ipairs(filtered) do
                if selected[option] then
                    return index
                end
            end
            return nil
        end
        local function move(delta)
            local total = #filtered
            if total == 0 then
                return
            end
            local target
            if active == nil then
                target = firstSelectedIndex() or (delta > 0 and 1 or total)
            else
                target = active + delta
                if math.abs(delta) == 1 then
                    if target < 1 then
                        target = total
                    elseif target > total then
                        target = 1
                    end
                else
                    target = math.clamp(target, 1, total)
                end
            end
            setActive(target, "key", true)
        end
        local function jumpTo(index)
            if #filtered > 0 then
                setActive(index, "key", true)
            end
        end
        local function activate()
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - lastActivate < 0.12 then
                return
            end
            lastActivate = now
            if active and filtered[active] ~= nil then
                toggleOption(filtered[active])
            end
        end
        local pageStep = math.max(1, maxRows - 1)

        local function applySelection(values)
            selected = {}
            for _, value in ipairs(values) do
                selected[value] = true
            end
        end
        selectAllButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local limit = tonumber(opt.MaxSelected)
            local count = limit and #getValue() or 0
            for _, option in ipairs(filtered) do
                if not selected[option] then
                    if limit and count >= limit then
                        break
                    end
                    selected[option] = true
                    count += 1
                end
            end
            commit()
        end)
        clearAllButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            selected = {}
            commit()
        end)
        searchBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
            ah(searchStroke, { Color = z.Stroke }, 0.2)
            ah(searchIconImage, { ImageColor3 = z.Muted }, 0.2)
            if enterPressed and by.Open then
                activate()
            end
        end)
        searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            if searchBox.Text == query then
                return
            end
            query = searchBox.Text
            tokens = tokenize(query)
            if not by.Open then
                return
            end
            rebuildFiltered()
            active = nil
            list.CanvasPosition = Vector2.zero
            invalidateRows()
            layoutPopup(false)
            renderRows(true)
            if #tokens > 0 and #filtered > 0 then
                setActive(1, "key", false)
            end
        end)

        function by:Set(value, silent)
            selected = {}
            if isMulti then
                applySelection(type(value) == "table" and value or { value })
            elseif value ~= nil then
                selected[value] = true
            end
            refreshDisplay(by.Open)
            aF(frameStroke)
            if not silent then
                aJ(opt.Callback, getValue())
            end
        end
        function by:Get()
            return getValue()
        end
        function by:GetOptions()
            return table.clone(options)
        end
        local function sameValue(x, y)
            if type(x) ~= "table" or type(y) ~= "table" then
                return x == y
            end
            if #x ~= #y then
                return false
            end
            for index, item in ipairs(x) do
                if y[index] ~= item then
                    return false
                end
            end
            return true
        end
        function by:Refresh(newOptions, keepSelection, silent)
            local before = getValue()
            setOptions(newOptions)
            if not keepSelection then
                selected = {}
            end
            rebuildFiltered()
            active = nil
            invalidateRows()
            refreshDisplay(false)
            if by.Open then
                layoutPopup(false)
                renderRows(true)
            end
            if not silent and not sameValue(before, getValue()) then
                aJ(opt.Callback, getValue())
            end
        end
        function by:_saveValue()
            local value = getValue()
            if isMulti then
                local seen = {}
                for _, option in ipairs(value) do
                    seen[option] = true
                end
                for option in pairs(selected) do
                    if not seen[option] then
                        table.insert(value, option)
                    end
                end
                return value
            end
            if value == nil then
                value = next(selected)
            end
            return value
        end
        function by:SetOpen(state)
            setOpen(state == true)
        end

        local canToggle = ay(button, function()
            return frame:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("ScrollingFrame")
        end)
        button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if canToggle() then
                setOpen(not by.Open)
            end
        end)
        button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if not by.Open and not ax() then
                ah(chipStroke, { Color = z.StrokeHover }, 0.15)
                ah(chip, { BackgroundColor3 = z.Surface2 }, 0.15)
            end
        end)
        button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            if not by.Open then
                ah(chipStroke, { Color = z.Stroke }, 0.25)
                ah(chip, { BackgroundColor3 = z.Surface }, 0.25)
            end
        end)
        by._listeners = {
            function()
                setOpen(false)
                unlockScroll()
                if renderConn then
                    renderConn()
                    renderConn = nil
                end
                popup:Destroy()
            end,
        }
        window:_listen("Began", function(input)
            if not by.Open then
                return
            end
            local code = input.KeyCode
            if code == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
                setOpen(false)
                return
            end
            if aw(input) then
                local pointer = av()
                if not az(pointer, popup) and not az(pointer, button) then
                    setOpen(false)
                end
                return
            end
            if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
                return
            end
            local typing = searchBox:IsFocused()
            if code == Enum.KeyCode.Down then
                move(1)
            elseif code == Enum.KeyCode.Up then
                move(-1)
            elseif code == Enum.KeyCode.PageDown then
                move(pageStep)
            elseif code == Enum.KeyCode.PageUp then
                move(-pageStep)
            elseif not typing and code == Enum.KeyCode.Home then
                jumpTo(1)
            elseif not typing and code == Enum.KeyCode.End then
                jumpTo(#filtered)
            elseif not typing and (code == Enum.KeyCode.Return or code == Enum.KeyCode.KeypadEnter or code == Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
                activate()
            end
        end, by)
        refreshDisplay(false)
        task.defer(resizeChip, true)
        return m(self, opt, by, frame, "Dropdown")
    end
    k._amountSuffixes = { k = 1e3, m = 1e6, b = 1e9, t = 1e12, qa = 1e15, qi = 1e18 }
    -- Parses "1.5k", "2,5m", "1,234.56", "1.234,56", "300kg", "12/s" ... into a number.
    -- Returns nil when the text is not a finite number.
    function k._parseAmount(text)
        text = string.lower(tostring(text)):gsub("[%s_%$]", "")
        text = text:gsub("kg$", ""):gsub("/s$", "")
        if text:find("^[%+%-]?0x") or text:find("^[%+%-]?0b[01]") then
            return nil
        end
        -- digits grouped by exactly three: "1,500" / "1.234.567" / "12,345k"
        local function grouped(sep)
            local head, tail = text:match("^[%+%-]?([1-9]%d?%d?)(.*)$")
            if not head then
                return false
            end
            local pos = 1
            while tail:sub(pos, pos) == sep and tail:sub(pos + 1, pos + 3):match("^%d%d%d$") do
                pos += 4
            end
            return pos > 1 and tail:sub(pos):match("^%a*$") ~= nil
        end
        local lastComma = text:find(",[^,]*$")
        local lastDot = text:find("%.[^%.]*$")
        if lastComma and lastDot then
            -- both marks present: the last one is the decimal mark
            if lastComma > lastDot then
                text = text:gsub("%.", ""):gsub(",", ".")
            else
                text = text:gsub(",", "")
            end
        elseif lastComma then
            local _, commaCount = text:gsub(",", "")
            if not grouped(",") and commaCount == 1 then
                text = text:gsub(",", ".") -- "1,5" / "2,50k": decimal comma
            else
                text = text:gsub(",", "")
            end
        elseif lastDot and grouped(".") then
            text = text:gsub("%.", "")
        end
        local value = tonumber(text)
        if value == nil then
            local number, unit = text:match("^(.-)(%a+)$")
            local mult = unit and k._amountSuffixes[unit]
            value = number and mult and tonumber(number)
            if value then
                value = value * mult
            end
        end
        if value == nil or value ~= value or value == math.huge or value == -math.huge then
            return nil
        end
        return value
    end
    -- Input: text box, or numeric box that understands 1.5k / 2m / 3b ... (Numeric = true).
    -- Options: Name/Title, Desc/Description, Default/CurrentValue, Placeholder/PlaceholderText, Icon, Flag, Tooltip, Visible,
    --          Disabled, MaxLength (characters), SelectOnFocus (default: Numeric), Numeric, Min/Max/Range (Numeric only),
    --          Callback(value, enterPressed) when the edit is committed, OnChanged(text) on every keystroke.
    -- Methods: Set(value, silent), Get(), SetDisabled(bool), IsDisabled(), SetText(title), SetDesc(text),
    --          SetPlaceholder(text), Focus(), ReleaseFocus(), IsFocused().
    -- Enter or clicking away commits, Escape cancels. Set() fires Callback when the value changed unless silent = true.
    function aS:Input(opts)
        local card, cardStroke, rowH, titleLbl, descLbl
        opts, card, cardStroke, rowH, titleLbl, descLbl = aQ(
            self,
            opts,
            { Title = "Name", Description = "Desc", PlaceholderText = "Placeholder", CurrentValue = "Default", Value = "Default" },
            "Frame",
            160,
            "Input"
        )
        local compact = aM(self).Compact
        local chipH = compact and aM(self).Chip + 2 or 30
        local numeric = opts.Numeric and true or false
        local maxLength = tonumber(opts.MaxLength)
        if maxLength then
            maxLength = math.floor(maxLength)
            if maxLength < 1 then
                maxLength = nil
            end
        end
        local minV, maxV = tonumber(opts.Min), tonumber(opts.Max)
        if minV and maxV and minV > maxV then
            minV, maxV = maxV, minV
        end
        local selectOnFocus = numeric
        if opts.SelectOnFocus ~= nil then
            selectOnFocus = opts.SelectOnFocus == true
        end
        local state = { disabled = opts.Disabled == true, focused = false, hover = false, invalid = false }
        local api = {}

        local function clampNumber(v)
            if minV and v < minV then
                return minV
            end
            if maxV and v > maxV then
                return maxV
            end
            return v
        end
        local function trimLength(text)
            if not maxLength then
                return text
            end
            local len = utf8.len(text)
            if not len then
                return text:sub(1, maxLength)
            end
            if len <= maxLength then
                return text
            end
            return text:sub(1, utf8.offset(text, maxLength + 1) - 1)
        end

        -- committedText / committedValue: the last accepted value (Numeric keeps the parsed number too)
        local initial = opts.Default ~= nil and trimLength(tostring(opts.Default)) or ""
        local committedText, committedValue = initial, nil
        if numeric then
            local raw = k._parseAmount(initial)
            if raw == nil then
                initial, committedText = "", ""
            else
                committedValue = clampNumber(raw)
                if committedValue ~= raw then
                    initial = tostring(committedValue)
                    committedText = initial
                end
            end
        end

        local chip = ai("Frame", {
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, compact and -14 or -10, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(170, chipH),
            BackgroundColor3 = z.Surface,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            ClipsDescendants = true,
            Parent = card,
        })
        aj(chip, UDim.new(0, 6))
        local chipStroke = ak(chip)
        local iconImage
        local hasIcon = opts.Icon ~= nil and opts.Icon ~= false
        if hasIcon then
            local iconFrame, image = aB(chip, opts.Icon, z.Muted, UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, 0))
            iconImage = image
        end
        local inset = hasIcon and 30 or 8
        local box = ai("TextBox", {
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(inset, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, hasIcon and -38 or -16, 1, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = initial,
            PlaceholderText = opts.Placeholder ~= nil and tostring(opts.Placeholder) or "",
            PlaceholderColor3 = z.Muted,
            TextColor3 = z.Text,
            TextSize = compact and 13 or 14,
            FontFace = D.Regular,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            ClearTextOnFocus = false,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None,
            ClipsDescendants = true,
            TextEditable = not state.disabled,
            Parent = chip,
        })
        -- invisible twin of the placeholder: TextBox.TextBounds ignores the placeholder, this lets the box fit it
        local ghost = ai("TextLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = box.PlaceholderText,
            TextSize = box.TextSize,
            FontFace = D.Regular,
            TextTransparency = 1,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None,
            Parent = chip,
        })

        -- Text written by code goes through setBoxText so the Text listener can tell it apart from typing.
        local lastText = initial
        local function setBoxText(text)
            lastText = text
            box.Text = text
        end

        local function paintStroke(dur)
            local color = z.Stroke
            if state.invalid then
                color = z.Error
            elseif state.focused or (state.hover and not state.disabled) then
                color = z.StrokeHover
            end
            ah(chipStroke, { Color = color }, dur)
        end
        local function paintDisabled(dur)
            local dim = state.disabled and 0.55 or 0
            ah(titleLbl, { TextTransparency = dim }, dur)
            if descLbl then
                ah(descLbl, { TextTransparency = dim }, dur)
            end
            ah(box, { TextTransparency = dim }, dur)
            if iconImage then
                ah(iconImage, { ImageTransparency = dim }, dur)
            end
        end

        local lastWidth, sizeTween
        local function fit(instant)
            local width
            if compact then
                width = aN(card, self.Window)
            else
                local cardW = card.AbsoluteSize.X / self.Window.Scale.Scale
                local maxW = math.clamp(cardW - 14 - 110 - 20, 100, 200)
                local textW = box.TextBounds.X
                if #box.Text == 0 then
                    textW = ghost.TextBounds.X
                end
                width = math.clamp(textW + inset + 8 + 12, 90, maxW) + (state.focused and 8 or 0)
                width = math.min(width, maxW + 8)
            end
            if width == lastWidth then
                return
            end
            lastWidth = width
            aR(titleLbl, descLbl, width + (compact and 24 or 20))
            if sizeTween then
                sizeTween:Cancel()
                sizeTween = nil
            end
            if instant then
                chip.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, chipH)
            else
                sizeTween = ah(chip, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, chipH) }, 0.18)
            end
        end
        local lastCardWidth = 0
        card:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
            if card.AbsoluteSize.X ~= lastCardWidth then
                lastCardWidth = card.AbsoluteSize.X
                fit(true)
            end
        end)
        box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextBounds"):Connect(function()
            fit(false)
        end)
        ghost:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextBounds"):Connect(function()
            fit(false)
        end)
        task.defer(fit, true)

        -- keystrokes: enforce MaxLength, flag unreadable numbers, notify OnChanged
        box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            local text = box.Text
            if text == lastText then
                return
            end
            if maxLength then
                local trimmed = trimLength(text)
                if trimmed ~= text then
                    text = trimmed
                    setBoxText(trimmed)
                end
            end
            lastText = text
            if numeric then
                local invalid = text ~= "" and k._parseAmount(text) == nil
                if invalid ~= state.invalid then
                    state.invalid = invalid
                    paintStroke(0.12)
                end
            end
            aJ(opts.OnChanged, text)
        end)

        local function commit(text, enterPressed)
            if numeric then
                local raw = k._parseAmount(text)
                if raw == nil then
                    setBoxText(committedText) -- not a number: put the previous value back
                    return
                end
                local value = clampNumber(raw)
                if value ~= raw then
                    text = tostring(value)
                    setBoxText(text)
                end
                committedText, committedValue = text, value
                aJ(opts.Callback, value, enterPressed)
                return
            end
            committedText = text
            aJ(opts.Callback, text, enterPressed)
        end

        box.Focused:Connect(function()
            state.focused = true
            paintStroke(0.15)
            fit(false)
            if selectOnFocus then
                task.defer(function()
                    if box:IsFocused() then
                        box.SelectionStart = 1
                        box.CursorPosition = #box.Text + 1
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
        box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed, inputObject)
            state.focused = false
            state.invalid = false
            paintStroke(0.15)
            if inputObject ~= nil and inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
                setBoxText(committedText)
            else
                commit(box.Text, enterPressed)
            end
            fit(false)
        end)
        box.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if ax() or state.disabled then
                return
            end
            state.hover = true
            paintStroke(0.12)
        end)
        box.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            state.hover = false
            paintStroke(0.25)
        end)

        function api:Set(value, silent)
            local text = value == nil and "" or tostring(value)
            text = trimLength(text)
            if numeric then
                local raw = k._parseAmount(text)
                if raw == nil then
                    return
                end
                local clamped = clampNumber(raw)
                if clamped ~= raw then
                    text = tostring(clamped)
                end
                local changed = clamped ~= committedValue
                committedText, committedValue = text, clamped
                setBoxText(text)
                aF(cardStroke)
                if changed and not silent then
                    aJ(opts.Callback, clamped, false)
                end
                return
            end
            local changed = text ~= committedText
            committedText = text
            setBoxText(text)
            aF(cardStroke)
            if changed and not silent then
                aJ(opts.Callback, text, false)
            end
        end
        function api:Get()
            if numeric then
                return committedValue
            end
            return box.Text
        end
        api._saveValue = numeric and function()
            return committedText
        end or nil
        function api:SetDisabled(v)
            state.disabled = v == true
            if state.disabled then
                state.hover = false
                if box:IsFocused() then
                    box:ReleaseFocus()
                end
            end
            box.TextEditable = not state.disabled
            paintDisabled(0.15)
            paintStroke(0.15)
        end
        function api:IsDisabled()
            return state.disabled
        end
        function api:SetText(text)
            text = tostring(text)
            titleLbl.Text = text
            api._searchName = text
            api._searchKey = string.lower(text)
        end
        function api:SetDesc(text)
            if descLbl then
                descLbl.Text = tostring(text)
            end
        end
        function api:SetPlaceholder(text)
            text = text ~= nil and tostring(text) or ""
            box.PlaceholderText = text
            ghost.Text = text
            fit(false)
        end
        function api:Focus()
            if not state.disabled then
                box:CaptureFocus()
            end
        end
        function api:ReleaseFocus()
            if box:IsFocused() then
                box:ReleaseFocus()
            end
        end
        function api:IsFocused()
            return box:IsFocused()
        end
        api._onHide = function()
            if box:IsFocused() then
                box:ReleaseFocus()
            end
        end

        paintDisabled(0)
        paintStroke(0)
        return m(self, opts, api, card, "Input")
    end
    function aS:Keybind(aU)
        local aV, aW, aX, aY, aZ
        aU, aV, aW, aX, aY, aZ = aQ(
            self,
            aU,
            { Title = "Name", Description = "Desc", CurrentKeybind = "Default", Value = "Default" },
            "Frame",
            110,
            "Keybind"
        )
        if type(aU.Default) == "string" then
            aU.Default = Enum.KeyCode[aU.Default]
        end
        local a_ = aM(self).Compact
        local a0 = aM(self).Chip
        local a1 = ai(
            "TextButton",
            {
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
                Position = UDim2.new(1, a_ and -14 or -10, 0.5, 0),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(44, a0),
                BackgroundColor3 = z.Surface,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Text = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                ClipsDescendants = true,
                Parent = aV,
            }
        )
        aj(a1, UDim.new(0, 6))
        local a2 = ak(a1)
        local a3 = am({
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
            TextSize = 13,
            TextColor3 = z.Muted,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None,
            Parent = a1,
        })
        local a4 = { Value = aU.Default, Listening = false }
        local function a5(a6)
            local a7 = math.max(a3.TextBounds.X + 20, E and 44 or 36)
            aR(aY, aZ, a7 + (a_ and 24 or 20))
            if a6 then
                a1.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(a7, a0)
            else
                ah(a1, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(a7, a0) }, 0.2)
            end
        end
        a3:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextBounds"):Connect(function()
            a5(false)
        end)
        local function a6()
            a3.Text = a4.Listening and "..." or aI(a4.Value)
            ah(a2, { Color = a4.Listening and z.StrokeHover or z.Stroke }, 0.15)
            ah(a3, { TextColor3 = a4.Listening and z.Accent or z.Muted }, 0.15)
        end
        function a4:Set(a7, a8)
            local a9 = a4.Listening
            a4.Value = a7
            a4.Listening = false
            a6()
            if not a9 then
                aF(aW)
            end
            if not a8 then
                aJ(aU.OnChanged, a7)
            end
        end
        a1.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            a4.Listening = not a4.Listening
            a6()
        end)
        self.Window:_listen("Began", function(a7, a8)
            if a7.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
                return
            end
            if a4.Listening then
                self.Window._consumedKey = a7.KeyCode
                self.Window._consumedAt = os.clock()
                if a7.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
                    a4.Listening = false
                    a6()
                else
                    a4:Set(a7.KeyCode)
                end
                return
            end
            if not a8 and a4.Value ~= nil and a7.KeyCode == a4.Value then
                aJ(aU.Callback, a7.KeyCode)
            end
        end, a4)
        a6()
        task.defer(a5, true)
        function a4:Get()
            return a4.Value
        end
        a4._onHide = function()
            if a4.Listening then
                a4.Listening = false
                a6()
            end
        end
        return m(self, aU, a4, aV, "Keybind")
    end
    function aS:ColorPicker(aU)
        local aV = 194
        local aW, aX, aY
        aU, aW, aX, aY = aQ(
            self,
            aU,
            { Title = "Name", Description = "Desc", Color = "Default", CurrentValue = "Default", Value = "Default" },
            "Frame"
        )
        aW.ClipsDescendants = true
        local aZ = aM(self)
        local a_ = aZ.Compact
        local a0 = ai(
            "TextButton",
            { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, aY), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, Parent = aW }
        )
        aP(a0, aU.Name or "Color", aU.Desc, 140, aZ)
        local a1 = ai(
            "Frame",
            {
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
                Position = UDim2.new(1, a_ and -38 or -34, 0.5, 0),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Parent = a0,
            }
        )
        aj(a1, UDim.new(0, 6))
        ak(a1, z.Stroke)
        local a2 = am({
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, a_ and -68 or -64, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(64, 16),
            TextSize = 12,
            FontFace = D.Regular,
            TextColor3 = z.Muted,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
            Parent = a0,
        })
        local a3 = aC(a0, 1, a_ and -14 or -12)
        local a4 = ai(
            "Frame",
            {
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, aY + 2),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, aV - 12),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Visible = false,
                Parent = aW,
            }
        )
        local a5 = ai(
            "TextButton",
            {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 118),
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Text = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Parent = a4,
            }
        )
        aj(a5, UDim.new(0, 8))
        ak(a5, z.Stroke)
        local a6 =
            ai("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Color3.new(1, 0, 0)), Parent = a5 })
        local a7 = ai(
            "Frame",
            { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = a5 }
        )
        aj(a7, UDim.new(0, 8))
        ai(
            "UIGradient",
            {
                Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0) }),
                Rotation = 90,
                Parent = a7,
            }
        )
        local function a8(a9, ba)
            local bb = ai(
                "Frame",
                {
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
                    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(ba, ba),
                    BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    ZIndex = 3,
                    Parent = a9,
                }
            )
            aj(bb, UDim.new(1, 0))
            ak(bb, Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.7)
            local bc = ai(
                "Frame",
                {
                    AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
                    Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 1, -4),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0,
                    ZIndex = 3,
                    Parent = bb,
                }
            )
            aj(bc, UDim.new(1, 0))
            return bb, bc
        end
        local a9, ba = a8(a5, 14)
        local bb = ai(
            "TextButton",
            {
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 132),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 10),
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Text = "",
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Parent = a4,
            }
        )
        aj(bb, UDim.new(1, 0))
        ai(
            "UIGradient",
            {
                Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1 / 6, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(2 / 6, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(3 / 6, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(4 / 6, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 255)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(5 / 6, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 255)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
                }),
                Parent = bb,
            }
        )
        local bc, bd = a8(bb, 16)
        bc.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5)
        local be = ai(
            "Frame",
            {
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 154),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(a_ and 110 or 124, 28),
                BackgroundColor3 = z.Surface,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Parent = a4,
            }
        )
        aj(be, UDim.new(0, 7))
        local bf = ak(be)
        local bg = ai(
            "Frame",
            {
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, 0),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Parent = be,
            }
        )
        aj(bg, UDim.new(0, 4))
        ak(bg, z.Stroke)
        local bh = ai(
            "TextBox",
            {
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 0),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -38, 1, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Text = "",
                TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None,
                ClipsDescendants = false,
                TextColor3 = z.Text,
                TextSize = 13,
                FontFace = D.Regular,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                ClearTextOnFocus = false,
                Parent = be,
            }
        )
        local bi = am({
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(a_ and 118 or 134, 154),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, a_ and -118 or -134, 0, 28),
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
            TextSize = 12,
            FontFace = D.Regular,
            TextColor3 = z.Muted,
            Parent = a4,
        })
        local bj = { Open = false }
        local bk, bl, bm = Color3.toHSV(aU.Default or z.Accent)
        local bn = nil
        local function bo(bp)
            return string.format(
                "#%02X%02X%02X",
                math.floor(bp.R * 255 + 0.5),
                math.floor(bp.G * 255 + 0.5),
                math.floor(bp.B * 255 + 0.5)
            )
        end
        local function bp(bq)
            local br = Color3.fromHSV(bk, bl, bm)
            bj.Value = br
            a1.BackgroundColor3 = br
            bg.BackgroundColor3 = br
            ba.BackgroundColor3 = br
            bd.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(bk, 1, 1)
            a2.Text = bo(br)
            a6.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Color3.fromHSV(bk, 1, 1))
            ah(a9, { Position = UDim2.fromScale(bl, 1 - bm) }, bq, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
            ah(bc, { Position = UDim2.fromScale(bk, 0.5) }, bq, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
            if not bh:IsFocused() then
                bh.Text = bo(br)
            end
            bi.Text = string.format(
                "%d  %d  %d",
                math.floor(br.R * 255 + 0.5),
                math.floor(br.G * 255 + 0.5),
                math.floor(br.B * 255 + 0.5)
            )
        end
        local function bq(br, bs)
            bp(br)
            if not bs then
                aJ(aU.Callback, bj.Value)
            end
        end
        function bj:Set(br, bs)
            bk, bl, bm = Color3.toHSV(br)
            bq(0.25, bs)
            aF(aX)
        end
        function bj:Get()
            return bj.Value
        end
        local function br(bs)
            bj.Open = bs
            ah(aW, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, bs and (aY + aV) or aY) }, 0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
            a3:Set(bs)
            if bs then
                a4.Visible = true
            else
                task.delay(0.35, function()
                    if not bj.Open then
                        a4.Visible = false
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        function bj:SetOpen(bs)
            br(bs == true)
        end
        a0.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            br(not bj.Open)
        end)
        local function bs(bt)
            bl = math.clamp((bt.X - a5.AbsolutePosition.X) / a5.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
            bm = 1 - math.clamp((bt.Y - a5.AbsolutePosition.Y) / a5.AbsoluteSize.Y, 0, 1)
            bq(0.04)
        end
        local function bt(bu)
            bk = math.clamp((bu.X - bb.AbsolutePosition.X) / bb.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 0.999)
            bq(0.04)
        end
        a5.InputBegan:Connect(function(bu)
            if aw(bu) then
                bn = "sv"
                ah(a9, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18) }, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
                bs(av())
            end
        end)
        bb.InputBegan:Connect(function(bu)
            if aw(bu) then
                bn = "hue"
                ah(bc, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20) }, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
                bt(av())
            end
        end)
        self.Window:_listen("Changed", function(bu)
            if not bn or not aA(bu) then
                return
            end
            local bv = av()
            if bn == "sv" then
                bs(bv)
            else
                bt(bv)
            end
        end, bj)
        self.Window:_listen("Ended", function(bu)
            if bn and aw(bu) then
                bn = nil
                ah(a9, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14) }, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
                ah(bc, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16) }, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
            end
        end, bj)
        bh.Focused:Connect(function()
            ah(bf, { Color = z.StrokeHover }, 0.15)
        end)
        bh.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            ah(bf, { Color = z.Stroke }, 0.15)
            local bu, bv, bw = bh.Text:match("^%s*#?(%x%x)(%x%x)(%x%x)%s*$")
            if bu then
                bj:Set(Color3.fromRGB(tonumber(bu, 16), tonumber(bv, 16), tonumber(bw, 16)))
            else
                bh.Text = bo(bj.Value)
            end
        end)
        bp(0)
        return m(self, aU, bj, aW, "ColorPicker")
    end
    -- Stepper: [-] value [+]. Click the value to type a number.
    -- Options: Name/Title, Desc/Description, Default/CurrentValue, Min, Max, Step/Increment, Suffix, Flag, Tooltip, Visible,
    --          Disabled, Callback(value).
    -- Methods: Set(value, silent), Get(), SetRange(min, max, step, silent), SetSuffix(text), SetDisabled(bool),
    --          IsDisabled(), SetText(title), SetDesc(text).
    -- Holding +/- repeats (and speeds up on wide ranges); Shift multiplies the step by 10 with a mouse.
    function aS:Stepper(opts)
        local card, cardStroke, rowH, titleLbl, descLbl
        opts, card, cardStroke, rowH, titleLbl, descLbl = aQ(
            self,
            opts,
            { Title = "Name", Description = "Desc", CurrentValue = "Default", Value = "Default", Increment = "Step" },
            "Frame",
            150,
            "Stepper"
        )
        local function finite(v, fallback)
            v = tonumber(v)
            if v == nil or v ~= v or v == math.huge or v == -math.huge then
                return fallback
            end
            return v
        end
        local minV = finite(opts.Min, 0)
        local maxV = finite(opts.Max, 100)
        if minV > maxV then
            minV, maxV = maxV, minV
        end
        local step = finite(opts.Step, 1)
        if not (step > 0) then
            step = 1
        end
        local suffix = (opts.Suffix ~= nil and opts.Suffix ~= false) and tostring(opts.Suffix) or ""
        local num = aL(minV, maxV, step)
        local compact = aM(self).Compact
        local chipH = aM(self).Chip
        local state = { disabled = opts.Disabled == true, editing = false, stepping = false }
        local api = { Value = num.snap(finite(opts.Default, minV)) }

        local chip = ai("Frame", {
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, compact and -14 or -10, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, chipH),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
            BackgroundColor3 = z.Surface,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Parent = card,
        })
        aj(chip, UDim.new(0, 6))
        local chipStroke = ak(chip)
        ai("UIListLayout", {
            FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
            SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
            VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center,
            Parent = chip,
        })
        local function makeButton(glyph, order)
            local button = ai("TextButton", {
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(chipH, chipH),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Text = glyph,
                TextColor3 = z.Muted,
                TextSize = 18,
                FontFace = D.Medium,
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                LayoutOrder = order,
                Parent = chip,
            })
            button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                if not state.disabled then
                    ah(button, { TextColor3 = z.Accent }, 0.12)
                end
            end)
            button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                ah(button, { TextColor3 = z.Muted }, 0.2)
            end)
            return button
        end
        local minusBtn = makeButton("\u{2212}", 1)
        local box = ai("TextBox", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 1, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Text = "",
            TextColor3 = z.Accent,
            TextSize = 13,
            FontFace = D.Medium,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None,
            ClearTextOnFocus = false,
            TextEditable = not state.disabled,
            LayoutOrder = 2,
            Parent = chip,
        })
        local plusBtn = makeButton("+", 3)

        local function paintStroke(dur)
            ah(chipStroke, { Color = state.editing and z.StrokeHover or z.Stroke }, dur)
        end
        local function paintLimits(dur)
            ah(minusBtn, { TextTransparency = (state.disabled or api.Value <= minV) and 0.6 or 0 }, dur)
            ah(plusBtn, { TextTransparency = (state.disabled or api.Value >= maxV) and 0.6 or 0 }, dur)
        end
        local function paintDisabled(dur)
            local dim = state.disabled and 0.55 or 0
            ah(titleLbl, { TextTransparency = dim }, dur)
            if descLbl then
                ah(descLbl, { TextTransparency = dim }, dur)
            end
            ah(box, { TextTransparency = dim }, dur)
            paintLimits(dur)
        end
        -- while typing the box shows the bare number, otherwise number + suffix
        local function refreshText()
            local text = num.format(api.Value)
            if not state.editing then
                text = text .. suffix
            end
            box.Text = text
        end

        local lastBoxWidth, sizeTween
        local function fit(instant)
            local width = math.max(box.TextBounds.X + 12, 30)
            if width == lastBoxWidth then
                return
            end
            lastBoxWidth = width
            if sizeTween then
                sizeTween:Cancel()
                sizeTween = nil
            end
            if instant then
                box.Size = UDim2.new(0, width, 1, 0)
            else
                sizeTween = ah(box, { Size = UDim2.new(0, width, 1, 0) }, 0.2)
            end
        end
        box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextBounds"):Connect(function()
            fit(state.editing)
        end)
        chip:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
            aR(titleLbl, descLbl, chip.AbsoluteSize.X / self.Window.Scale.Scale + (compact and 24 or 20))
        end)

        function api:Set(value, silent)
            value = tonumber(value)
            if not value or value ~= value then
                return
            end
            value = num.snap(value)
            if value == api.Value then
                return
            end
            api.Value = value
            refreshText()
            paintLimits(0.15)
            if not state.stepping then
                aF(cardStroke)
            end
            if not silent then
                aJ(opts.Callback, value)
            end
        end
        function api:Get()
            return api.Value
        end

        -- next value on the step grid in `direction`; `count` steps at once (the range ends are always reachable)
        local function stepTarget(direction, count)
            local index = (api.Value - minV) / step
            if direction > 0 then
                index = math.floor(index + 1e-6) + count
            else
                index = math.ceil(index - 1e-6) - count
            end
            return num.snap(minV + index * step)
        end
        local function nudge(direction, count)
            if state.disabled or api._destroyed then
                return false
            end
            local target = stepTarget(direction, count)
            if target == api.Value then
                return false
            end
            state.stepping = true
            api:Set(target)
            state.stepping = false
            ah(chipStroke, { Color = z.StrokeHover }, 0.08)
            task.delay(0.12, function()
                if not api._destroyed then
                    paintStroke(0.2)
                end
            end)
            return true
        end
        local function shiftMultiplier()
            local ok, down = pcall(function()
                return b:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) or b:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.RightShift)
            end)
            return ok and down and 10 or 1
        end
        -- long holds on wide ranges move in bigger strides
        local function holdMultiplier(ticks)
            if (maxV - minV) / step < 50 then
                return 1
            end
            if ticks >= 40 then
                return 10
            elseif ticks >= 20 then
                return 4
            end
            return 1
        end
        local function bindHold(button, direction)
            local held, holdId, repeated = false, 0, false
            local canTap = ay(button, function()
                return button:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("ScrollingFrame")
            end)
            button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if not aw(input) or state.disabled then
                    return
                end
                local touch = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
                held, repeated = true, false
                holdId += 1
                local myId = holdId
                if not touch then
                    nudge(direction, shiftMultiplier())
                end
                task.delay(0.4, function()
                    if not (held and holdId == myId) or not canTap() then
                        return
                    end
                    repeated = true
                    local ticks = 0
                    while held and holdId == myId and canTap() do
                        if not nudge(direction, math.max(holdMultiplier(ticks), shiftMultiplier())) then
                            break
                        end
                        ticks += 1
                        task.wait(ticks >= 20 and 0.05 or 0.07)
                    end
                end)
            end)
            button.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
                if not aw(input) then
                    return
                end
                -- a touch that started a page scroll must not change the value; a plain tap steps on release
                if held and not repeated and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and canTap() then
                    nudge(direction, 1)
                end
                held = false
            end)
            button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                held = false
            end)
        end
        bindHold(minusBtn, -1)
        bindHold(plusBtn, 1)

        -- typing a value: "12", "12.5", "12,5", or with the suffix ("50%")
        local function parseTyped(text)
            text = tostring(text):gsub(",", "."):gsub("%s", "")
            local bare = suffix:gsub("%s", "")
            if bare ~= "" and text:lower():sub(-#bare) == bare:lower() then
                text = text:sub(1, #text - #bare)
            end
            return tonumber(text:match("^[%+%-]?%d*%.?%d*$"))
        end
        box.Focused:Connect(function()
            if state.disabled then
                box:ReleaseFocus()
                return
            end
            state.editing = true
            refreshText()
            paintStroke(0.12)
            fit(true)
            task.defer(function()
                if box:IsFocused() then
                    box.SelectionStart = 1
                    box.CursorPosition = #box.Text + 1
                end
            end)
        end)
        box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed, inputObject)
            local cancelled = inputObject ~= nil and inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape
            local typed = box.Text
            state.editing = false
            if not cancelled then
                local parsed = parseTyped(typed)
                if parsed then
                    api:Set(parsed)
                end
            end
            refreshText()
            paintStroke(0.2)
            fit(false)
        end)

        function api:SetRange(newMin, newMax, newStep, silent)
            newMin = finite(newMin, minV)
            newMax = finite(newMax, maxV)
            if newMin > newMax then
                newMin, newMax = newMax, newMin
            end
            minV, maxV = newMin, newMax
            newStep = finite(newStep, nil)
            if newStep and newStep > 0 then
                step = newStep
            end
            num = aL(minV, maxV, step)
            local clamped = num.snap(api.Value)
            local changed = clamped ~= api.Value
            api.Value = clamped
            refreshText()
            paintLimits(0.15)
            if changed and not silent then
                aJ(opts.Callback, clamped)
            end
        end
        function api:SetSuffix(text)
