            suffix = text ~= nil and tostring(text) or ""
            refreshText()
        end
        function api:SetDisabled(v)
            state.disabled = v == true
            if state.disabled and box:IsFocused() then
                box:ReleaseFocus()
            end
            box.TextEditable = not state.disabled
            paintDisabled(0.15)
        end
        function api:IsDisabled()
            return state.disabled
        end
        function api:SetText(text)
            text = tostring(text)
            titleLbl.Text = text
            api._searchName = text
            api._searchKey = string.lower(text)
        end
        function api:SetDesc(text)
            if descLbl then
                descLbl.Text = tostring(text)
            end
        end
        api._onHide = function()
            if box:IsFocused() then
                box:ReleaseFocus()
            end
        end

        refreshText()
        paintDisabled(0)
        paintStroke(0)
        task.defer(fit, true)
        return m(self, opts, api, card, "Stepper")
    end
    -- Shared helpers for Progress / Status / StatusList / Table.
    -- Stored on `k` (not as new locals) because the enclosing function is close to Luau's 200-local limit.
    -- Names start with "_" so the public-API proxy at the end of the file leaves them unwrapped.
    function k._formatNumber(value, decimals, separator, abbreviate)
        if type(value) ~= "number" or value ~= value or value == math.huge or value == -math.huge then
            return tostring(value)
        end
        local suffix = ""
        if abbreviate then
            local units = { "K", "M", "B", "T", "Qa" }
            local scaled = value
            local step = 0
            while math.abs(scaled) >= 1000 and step < #units do
                scaled = scaled / 1000
                step = step + 1
            end
            if step > 0 then
                value = scaled
                suffix = units[step]
                if decimals == nil then
                    decimals = 1
                end
            end
        end
        local text
        if decimals ~= nil then
            text = string.format("%." .. math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(decimals) or 0), 0, 8) .. "f", value)
        elseif value == math.floor(value) and math.abs(value) < 1e15 then
            text = string.format("%d", value)
        else
            text = string.format("%.2f", value)
            text = text:gsub("0+$", "")
            text = text:gsub("%.$", "")
        end
        if suffix ~= "" and text:find(".", 1, true) then
            text = text:gsub("0+$", "")
            text = text:gsub("%.$", "")
        end
        if separator ~= false then
            local sign, whole, rest = text:match("^(%-?)(%d+)(.*)$")
            if whole and #whole > 3 then
                whole = whole:reverse():gsub("(%d%d%d)", "%1,"):reverse():gsub("^,", "")
                text = sign .. whole .. rest
            end
        end
        return text .. suffix
    end
    -- Colour thresholds: { { 0, "Error" }, { 0.35, "Warning" }, { 0.7, "Success" } } (fraction 0..1, ascending).
    -- `preset` ("auto" / "inverse") builds a ready-made list when no explicit list is given.
    function k._buildStops(source, preset)
        if source == nil and preset then
            if type(preset) == "string" and (string.lower(preset) == "inverse" or string.lower(preset) == "reverse") then
                source = { { 0, "Success" }, { 0.67, "Warning" }, { 0.9, "Error" } }
            else
                source = { { 0, "Error" }, { 0.34, "Warning" }, { 0.67, "Success" } }
            end
        end
        local stops = {}
        if type(source) == "table" then
            for index, entry in ipairs(source) do
                if type(entry) == "table" then
                    local at = tonumber(entry.At or entry.From or entry[1])
                    local color = entry.Color
                    if color == nil then
                        color = entry.Tone
                    end
                    if color == nil then
                        color = entry[2]
                    end
                    if at ~= nil and color ~= nil then
                        stops[#stops + 1] = { At = at, Color = color }
                    end
                end
            end
            table.sort(stops, function(left, right)
                return left.At < right.At
            end)
        end
        return stops
    end
    function k._pickStop(stops, fraction)
        local picked = nil
        for index = 1, #stops do
            if fraction >= stops[index].At then
                picked = stops[index].Color
            else
                break
            end
        end
        return picked
    end
    -- True while `element` (living in `owner`, a tab / sub-tab / groupbox) can actually be seen by the user.
    function k._isLive(owner, window, element)
        if window._destroyed or not window.Open or window.Minimized or element._userVisible == false then
            return false
        end
        if owner._groupbox then
            if owner._userVisible == false then
                return false
            end
            owner = owner.Parent
        end
        if owner._isSubTab then
            if owner.Parent.CurrentSubTab ~= owner then
                return false
            end
            owner = owner.Parent
        end
        return window.CurrentTab == owner
    end
    -- Runs `step` immediately, then every `getRate()` seconds while `canRun()` allows it, until the element is destroyed.
    function k._poll(element, frame, getRate, canRun, step)
        local running = true
        element._listeners = element._listeners or {}
        table.insert(element._listeners, function()
            running = false
        end)
        task.defer(function()
            local first = true
            while running and frame.Parent do
                if first or canRun() then
                    first = false
                    step()
                end
                task.wait(getRate())
            end
        end)
    end
    function aS:Progress(options)
        options = l(options, { Title = "Name", Description = "Desc", CurrentValue = "Default", Value = "Default" })
        local metrics = aM(self)
        local compact = metrics.Compact
        local window = self.Window
        local card, cardStroke =
            aO(self, "Frame", (options.Desc and metrics.DescHeight or metrics.Height) + (compact and 6 or 10), options)
        aG(card, cardStroke)
        local textTop
        if options.Desc then
            textTop = math.floor((metrics.DescHeight - (metrics.DescGap + metrics.DescLine)) / 2) - 2
        else
            textTop = compact and 5 or 12
        end
        local titleLabel = am({
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, textTop),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 18),
            Text = tostring(options.Name or "Progress"),
            TextSize = metrics.TextSize,
            Parent = card,
        })
        local descLabel
        if options.Desc then
            descLabel = am({
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, textTop + metrics.DescGap),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, metrics.DescLine),
                Text = tostring(options.Desc),
                TextSize = metrics.DescSize,
                FontFace = D.Regular,
                TextColor3 = z.Muted,
                Parent = card,
            })
        end
        local valueLabel = am({
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, textTop),
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 18),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
            TextSize = 13,
            TextColor3 = z.Accent,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None,
            RichText = true,
            Parent = card,
        })
        -- the title always keeps the room the value text needs (custom Format strings can be long)
        local function fitTitle()
            local cardWidth = card.AbsoluteSize.X / math.max(window.Scale.Scale, 0.01)
            local valueWidth = math.min(valueLabel.TextBounds.X, math.floor(cardWidth * 0.55))
            titleLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(36 + math.ceil(valueWidth)), 0, 18)
        end
        valueLabel:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextBounds"):Connect(fitTitle)
        card:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(fitTitle)
        local track = ai("Frame", {
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 1, compact and -12 or -16),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 5),
            BackgroundColor3 = z.Surface3,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            ClipsDescendants = true,
            Parent = card,
        })
        aj(track, UDim.new(1, 0))
        local fill = ai("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
            BackgroundColor3 = z.Accent,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Parent = track,
        })
        aj(fill, UDim.new(1, 0))
        local sweep = ai("Frame", {
            Position = UDim2.fromScale(-0.4, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.4, 1),
            BackgroundColor3 = z.Accent,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Visible = false,
            Parent = track,
        })
        aj(sweep, UDim.new(1, 0))

        local api = { _listeners = {} }
        local minValue = tonumber(options.Min) or 0
        local maxValue = tonumber(options.Max) or 1
        if maxValue <= minValue then
            maxValue = minValue + 1
        end
        local function clampValue(value)
            return math.clamp(tonumber(value) or minValue, minValue, maxValue)
        end
        local function toFraction(value)
            return math.clamp((value - minValue) / (maxValue - minValue), 0, 1)
        end
        api.Value = clampValue(options.Default)
        local barColorSpec = options.Color
        if barColorSpec == nil then
            barColorSpec = options.Tone
        end
        local textColorSpec = options.TextColor
        local formatFunction = options.Format
        local stops = k._buildStops(options.Thresholds, options.AutoColor)
        local indeterminate = options.Indeterminate == true
        local sweepTween = nil

        local function barColor(fraction)
            local spec = barColorSpec
            if spec == nil and #stops > 0 then
                spec = k._pickStop(stops, fraction)
            end
            return k._color(spec) or z.Accent
        end
        local function render(duration)
            local fraction = toFraction(api.Value)
            local color = barColor(fraction)
            local colorTime = duration > 0 and 0.25 or 0
            if indeterminate then
                ah(sweep, { BackgroundColor3 = color }, colorTime)
            else
                ah(fill, { Size = UDim2.new(fraction, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = color }, duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
            end
            local text
            if indeterminate then
                text = tostring(options.IndeterminateText or "...")
            else
                text = string.format("%d%%", math.floor(fraction * 100 + 0.5))
                if type(formatFunction) == "function" then
                    local ok, result = pcall(formatFunction, api.Value, fraction, minValue, maxValue)
                    if ok and result ~= nil then
                        text = tostring(result)
                    end
                end
            end
            valueLabel.Text = k._rich(text)
            local labelColor
            if textColorSpec ~= nil then
                labelColor = k._color(textColorSpec) or z.Accent
            elseif barColorSpec == nil and #stops > 0 then
                labelColor = color
            else
                labelColor = z.Accent
            end
            ah(valueLabel, { TextColor3 = labelColor }, colorTime)
        end
        local function setIndeterminate(enabled)
            enabled = enabled and true or false
            if sweepTween then
                sweepTween:Cancel()
                sweepTween = nil
            end
            indeterminate = enabled
            fill.Visible = not enabled
            sweep.Visible = enabled
            if enabled then
                sweep.Position = UDim2.fromScale(-0.4, 0)
                sweepTween = a:Create(
                    sweep,
                    TweenInfo.new(1.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1),
                    { Position = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0) }
                )
                sweepTween:Play()
            end
            render(0)
        end

        function api:Set(value, silent)
            value = clampValue(value)
            if value == api.Value then
                return
            end
            api.Value = value
            render(0.35)
            if not silent then
                aJ(options.Callback, value, toFraction(value))
            end
        end
        function api:Get()
            return api.Value
        end
        function api:GetFraction()
            return toFraction(api.Value)
        end
        function api:GetPercent()
            return toFraction(api.Value) * 100
        end
        function api:Add(delta, silent)
            api:Set(api.Value + (tonumber(delta) or 0), silent)
        end
        function api:SetRange(newMin, newMax)
            newMin = tonumber(newMin) or minValue
            newMax = tonumber(newMax) or maxValue
            if newMax <= newMin then
                newMax = newMin + 1
            end
            minValue, maxValue = newMin, newMax
            api.Value = clampValue(api.Value)
            render(0.25)
        end
        function api:SetColor(color)
            barColorSpec = color
            render(0.25)
        end
        function api:SetTextColor(color)
            textColorSpec = color
            render(0.25)
        end
        function api:SetThresholds(source)
            stops = k._buildStops(source, nil)
            render(0.25)
        end
        function api:SetFormat(callback)
            formatFunction = callback
            render(0)
        end
        function api:SetName(text)
            titleLabel.Text = tostring(text == nil and "" or text)
            api._searchName = titleLabel.Text
            api._searchKey = string.lower(titleLabel.Text)
        end
        function api:SetDescription(text)
            if descLabel then
                descLabel.Text = tostring(text == nil and "" or text)
            end
        end
        function api:SetIndeterminate(enabled)
            setIndeterminate(enabled)
        end
        function api:IsIndeterminate()
            return indeterminate
        end
        table.insert(api._listeners, function()
            if sweepTween then
                sweepTween:Cancel()
                sweepTween = nil
            end
        end)

        local updateRate = math.max(tonumber(options.UpdateRate) or 1, 0.05)
        function api:SetUpdateRate(rate)
            updateRate = math.max(tonumber(rate) or updateRate, 0.05)
        end
        if type(options.Update) == "function" then
            k._poll(api, card, function()
                return updateRate
            end, function()
                return options.AlwaysUpdate == true or k._isLive(self, window, api)
            end, function()
                local ok, value, newMax = aK(options.Update)
                if not ok then
                    warn("[AirFlow] progress update error: " .. tostring(value))
                    return
                end
                if type(newMax) == "number" and newMax ~= maxValue then
                    api:SetRange(minValue, newMax)
                end
                if value ~= nil then
                    api:Set(value, true)
                end
            end)
        end

        ag(valueLabel, function()
            render(0)
        end)
        if indeterminate then
            setIndeterminate(true)
        end
        return m(self, options, api, card, "Progress")
    end
    function aS:OrderList(aU)
        aU = l(aU, { Title = "Name", Description = "Desc", Options = "Items", Values = "Items" })

        local a1 = E and 30 or 22

        am({ Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18), Text = a6, TextSize = aV.TextSize, Visible = a6 ~= "", LayoutOrder = 1, Parent = a5 })
        if aU.Desc then
            am({

            "ScrollingFrame",

                BorderSizePixel = 0,

            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, aY),

        local function ba(bb, bc, bd, be, bf)
            bf.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            bf.Text = ""

            bf.Parent = bb

            end
            bh:Destroy()

        local function bb(bc, bd, be, bf)

        end
        local bc = {}
        local bd = {}
        local be = {}
        local bf = nil
        local bg, bh
        local function bi(bj)

        end
        local function bj()

            for bl, bm in ipairs(bd) do
                bk[bl] = bm.Value
            end

        end
        local function bk(bl)

            for bn, bo in ipairs(bd) do

                end

                    ah(bo.UpIcon.Object, { [bo.UpIcon.Fade] = bn == 1 and 0.7 or 0 }, bl and 0 or 0.15)
                    ah(bo.DownIcon.Object, { [bo.DownIcon.Fade] = bn == bm and 0.7 or 0 }, bl and 0 or 0.15)
                end

        local function bl()

            local bn = math.max(bm, 1) * a_ - aZ + 2

            local bq = bm > a2

                a7.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, br)

        end
        local function bm(bn)

            if not bn then

            end
        end
        local function bn()

        local function bo(bp)

                "Frame",

                    local bB = ay(by, bn)

                        if not ax() then

                        ah(bA.Object, { [bA.Color] = z.Muted }, 0.2)

                    by.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                        if bB() then
                            bg(br, bz)
                        end
                    end)
                    table.insert(bv, by)
                end
            end
            bs.InputBegan:Connect(function(bw)
                if aw(bw) then
                    bh(br)
                end
            end)
            bq.InputBegan:Connect(function(bw)
                if bw.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    return
                end

                for by, bz in ipairs(bv) do
                    if az(bx, bz) then

                    end

                bh(br)
            end)
            bq.MouseEnter:Connect(function()

                    bb(br.GripIcon, z.Text, 0, 0.12)

        local function bp(bq)
            local br = {}
            for bs, bt in ipairs(bd) do
                br[bt.Value] = br[bt.Value] or {}
                table.insert(br[bt.Value], bt)
            end
            local bs = {}
            for bt, bu in ipairs(bq) do
                local bv = br[bu]
                local bw = bv and table.remove(bv, 1)
                if not bw then
                    bw = bo(bu)
                    bw.Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(3, bi(bt))
                end
                bs[bt] = bw
            end
            for bt, bu in pairs(br) do
                for bv, bw in ipairs(bu) do
                    bw.Frame:Destroy()
                end
            end
            bd = bs
        end
        local function bq(br, bs)
            local bt = {}
            for bu, bv in ipairs(br) do
                bt[bv] = (bt[bv] or 0) + 1
            end
            local bu = {}
            for bv, bw in ipairs({ bs, br }) do
                for bx, by in ipairs(bw) do
                    if (bt[by] or 0) > 0 then
                        bt[by] -= 1
                        table.insert(bu, by)
                    end
                end
            end
            return bu
        end
        local function br(bs)
            local bt = bi(bs) - 1
            local bu = a7.AbsoluteWindowSize.Y / aX.Scale.Scale
            local bv = a7.CanvasPosition.Y
            if bt < bv then
                bv = bt
            elseif bt + aY + 2 > bv + bu then
                bv = bt + aY + 2 - bu
            end
            if bv ~= a7.CanvasPosition.Y then
                ah(a7, { CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(bv, 0)) }, 0.2)
            end
        end
        function bg(bs, bt)
            if bf then
                return
            end
            local bu = table.find(bd, bs)
            local bv = bu and bu + bt
            if not bv or bv < 1 or bv > #bd then
                return
            end
            bd[bu], bd[bv] = bd[bv], bd[bu]
            bk(false)
            ah(bs.Stroke, { Color = z.Accent }, 0.08)
            task.delay(0.18, function()
                if bs ~= bf then
                    ah(bs.Stroke, { Color = z.Stroke }, 0.3)
                end
            end)
            br(bv)
            bm(false)
        end
        local bs, bt = 0, nil
        local bu, bv, bw = nil, nil, nil
        local function bx(by)
            local bz = aX.Scale.Scale
            local bA = (by.Y - bs - a7.AbsolutePosition.Y) / bz + a7.CanvasPosition.Y
            bA = math.clamp(bA, bi(1), bi(#bd))
            bf.Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(3, bA)
            local bB = math.clamp(math.floor((bA - 1) / a_ + 0.5) + 1, 1, #bd)
            local bC = table.find(bd, bf)
            if bC ~= bB then
                table.remove(bd, bC)
                table.insert(bd, bB, bf)
                bk(false)
            end
        end
        local function by(bz)
            if not bf or not bt then
                return
            end
            local bA = aX.Scale.Scale
            local bB = a7.AbsolutePosition.Y
            local bC = bB + a7.AbsoluteSize.Y
            local bD = aY * bA
            local bE = 0
            if bt.Y < bB + bD then
                bE = -(bB + bD - bt.Y) / bD
            elseif bt.Y > bC - bD then
                bE = (bt.Y - (bC - bD)) / bD
            end
            if bE == 0 then
                return
            end
            local bF = math.max(a7.CanvasSize.Y.Offset - a7.AbsoluteWindowSize.Y / bA, 0)
            local bG = math.clamp(a7.CanvasPosition.Y + math.clamp(bE, -1.5, 1.5) * 360 * bz, 0, bF)
            if bG ~= a7.CanvasPosition.Y then
                a7.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, bG)
                bx(bt)
            end
        end
        function bh(bz)
            if bf or #bd < 2 or bc._destroyed then
                return
            end
            bf = bz
            bu = table.clone(bd)
            bt = av()
            bs = bt.Y - bz.Frame.AbsolutePosition.Y
            bz.Frame.ZIndex = 5
            ah(bz.Frame, { BackgroundColor3 = z.Surface3 }, 0.12)
            ah(bz.Stroke, { Color = z.Accent }, 0.12)
            ah(bz.Scale, { Scale = 1.02 }, 0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
            bb(bz.GripIcon, z.Accent, 0, 0.12)
            a7.ScrollingEnabled = false
            local bA = a5:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("ScrollingFrame")
            if bA and bA.ScrollingEnabled then
                bw = bA
                bA.ScrollingEnabled = false
            end
            bv = aX:_listen("Render", by)
        end
        local function bz(bA)
            local bB = bf
            if not bB then
                return
            end
            bf = nil
            bt = nil
            if bv then
                bv()
                bv = nil
            end
            if bw then
                bw.ScrollingEnabled = true
                bw = nil
            end
            ah(bB.Frame, { BackgroundColor3 = z.Surface }, 0.2)
            ah(bB.Stroke, { Color = z.Stroke }, 0.25)
            ah(bB.Scale, { Scale = 1 }, 0.2)
            bb(bB.GripIcon, z.Muted, 0, 0.25)
            task.delay(0.22, function()
                if bB ~= bf then
                    bB.Frame.ZIndex = 1
                end
            end)
            bk(false)
            bl()
            for bC, bD in ipairs(bd) do
                if bu[bC] ~= bD then
                    bm(bA)
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        aX:_listen("Changed", function(bA)
            if bf and aA(bA) then
                bt = av()
                bx(bt)
            end
        end, bc)
        aX:_listen("Ended", function(bA)
            if bf and aw(bA) then
                bz(false)
            end
        end, bc)
        table.insert(bc._listeners, function()
            bz(true)
        end)
        bc._onHide = function()
            bz(false)
        end
        function bc:Set(bA, bB)
            if type(bA) ~= "table" then
                return
            end
            bz(true)
            be = table.clone(bA)
            bp(bq(bj(), be))
            bk(false)
            bl()
            bm(bB)
        end
        function bc:Get()
            return bj()
        end
        function bc:Refresh(bA, bB, bC)
            bz(true)
            bA = type(bA) == "table" and table.clone(bA) or {}
            if bB ~= false then
                bA = bq(bA, bc:_saveValue())
            else
                be = {}
            end
            bp(bA)
            bk(false)
            bl()
            bm(bC)
        end
        function bc:_saveValue()
            local bA = bj()
            local bB = {}
            for bC, bD in ipairs(bA) do
                bB[bD] = (bB[bD] or 0) + 1
            end
            for bC, bD in ipairs(be) do
                if (bB[bD] or 0) > 0 then
                    bB[bD] -= 1
                else
                    table.insert(bA, bD)
                end
            end
            return bA
        end
        bp(type(aU.Items) == "table" and aU.Items or {})
        bk(true)
        bl()
        bc.Value = bj()
        return m(self, aU, bc, a5, "OrderList")
    end
    for aU, aV in pairs(table.clone(aS)) do
        if type(aV) == "function" and aU:sub(1, 1) ~= "_" and aU:sub(1, 6) ~= "Create" then
            aS["Create" .. aU] = aV
        end
    end
    local function aU()
        local aV
        pcall(function()
            if typeof(identifyexecutor) == "function" then
                aV = (identifyexecutor())
            elseif typeof(getexecutorname) == "function" then
                aV = getexecutorname()
            end
        end)
        if type(aV) == "string" and #aV > 0 then
            return aV
        end
        return d:IsStudio() and "Studio" or "Unknown"
    end
    local function aV(aW)
        local aX = aU()
        local aY = aW.SupportedExecutors
        if type(aY) == "table" then
            local aZ = string.lower(aX)
            for a_, a0 in ipairs(aY) do
                if string.find(aZ, string.lower(tostring(a0)), 1, true) then
                    return aX, true, "Your executor is supported and fully compatible."
                end
            end
            return aX, false, "Not on the supported list. Some features may not work."
        end
        local aZ = {
            { "writefile", writefile },
            { "readfile", readfile },
            { "getcustomasset", getcustomasset },
            { "setclipboard", setclipboard or toclipboard },
            { "request", request or http_request or (syn and syn.request) },
        }
        local a_ = {}
        for a0, a1 in ipairs(aZ) do
            if type(a1[2]) ~= "function" then
                table.insert(a_, a1[1])
            end
        end
        if #a_ == 0 then
            return aX, true, "Your executor is supported and fully compatible."
        end
        return aX, false, "Missing " .. table.concat(a_, ", ") .. ". Some features may not work."
    end
    local aW = {}
    function aW.Path(aX)
        return tostring(aX) .. "/unsupported_skip.txt"
    end
    function aW.Available()
        return type(writefile) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function" and type(isfile) == "function"
    end
    function aW.Saved(aX, aY)
        if not aW.Available() then
            return false
        end
        local aZ = aW.Path(aX)
        local a_, a0 = pcall(function()
            return isfile(aZ) and readfile(aZ) or nil
        end)
        return a_ and a0 == aY
    end
    function aW.Write(aX, aY, aZ)
        if not aW.Available() then
            return false
        end
        return pcall(function()
            if type(isfolder) == "function" and type(makefolder) == "function" then
                local a_ = ""
                for a0 in tostring(aX):gmatch("[^/\\]+") do
                    a_ = a_ == "" and a0 or a_ .. "/" .. a0
                    if not isfolder(a_) then
                        makefolder(a_)
                    end
                end
            end
            writefile(aY, aZ)
        end)
    end
    function aW.Save(aX, aY)
        return aW.Write(aX, aW.Path(aX), aY)
    end
    function aW.DisclaimerKey(aX)
        if aX.Id ~= nil then
            return (tostring(aX.Id):gsub("[\r\n]", " "))
        end
        local aY = tostring(aX.Title) .. "\0" .. tostring(aX.Text)
        local aZ = 5381
        for a_ = 1, #aY do
            aZ = (aZ * 33 + aY:byte(a_)) % 4294967296
        end
        return string.format("%08x", aZ)
    end
    function aW.DisclaimerAccepted(aX, aY)
        if not aW.Available() then
            return false
        end
        local aZ = tostring(aX) .. "/disclaimers.txt"
        local a_, a0 = pcall(function()
            return isfile(aZ) and readfile(aZ) or ""
        end)
        if not a_ or type(a0) ~= "string" then
            return false
        end
        for a1 in a0:gmatch("[^\r\n]+") do
            if a1 == aY then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    function aW.AcceptDisclaimer(aX, aY)
        if aW.DisclaimerAccepted(aX, aY) then
            return true
        end
        local aZ = tostring(aX) .. "/disclaimers.txt"
        local a_, a0 = pcall(function()
            return isfile(aZ) and readfile(aZ) or ""
        end)
        a0 = a_ and type(a0) == "string" and a0 or ""
        if a0 ~= "" and not a0:match("\n$") then
            a0 ..= "\n"
        end
        return aW.Write(aX, aZ, a0 .. aY .. "\n")
    end
    local aX = nil
    local function aY()
        if aX == nil then
            local aZ, a_ = pcall(function()
                return h:GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId)
            end)
            aX = aZ and type(a_) == "table" and a_ or false
        end
        return aX or nil
    end
    local function aZ()

        end
        table.sort(a7, function(a8, a9)
            return a8.LayoutOrder < a9.LayoutOrder
        end)
        return a7
    end
    local function a6(a7, a8, a9)
        if not a7.Visible and not a9 then
            return
        end
        a7.Visible = false
        task.delay(a8, function()
            if not a7.Parent then
                return
            end
            local ba = ai("UIScale", { Scale = 0.94, Parent = a7 })
            a7.Visible = true
            ah(ba, { Scale = 1 }, 0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
            task.delay(0.4, function()
                ba:Destroy()
            end)
        end)
    end
    function a2:_revealCards(a7, a8)
        local a9 = a7.CurrentSubTab or a7
        if a9._revealed then
            return
        end
        a9._revealed = true
        local ba = 0
        local function bb(bc)
            a6(bc, (a8 or 0) + ba * 0.035)
            ba += 1
        end
        local bc = a9._columnSet
        for bd, be in ipairs(a5(a9.List)) do
            if bc and be == bc.Holder then
                local bf, bg = a5(bc.Left), a5(bc.Right)
                for bh = 1, math.max(#bf, #bg) do
                    if bf[bh] then
                        bb(bf[bh])
                    end
                    if bg[bh] then
                        bb(bg[bh])
                    end
                end
            else
                bb(be)
            end
        end
    end
    function a2:_playIntro(a7)
        if self._introDone or self._destroyed then
            return
        end
        self._introDone = true
        self:_refreshBackdrop(true)
        task.delay(0.4, function()
            self:_refreshToggleButton()
        end)
        task.delay(1.5, function()
            if not self._destroyed and not self.MiniBar then
                self:_buildMiniBar()
            end
        end)
        local a8, a9, ba = self.Root, self.Shadow, self.Scale
        if self.CurrentTab then
            self:_revealCards(self.CurrentTab, a7 and 0.15 or 0.25)
        end
        a8.Visible = true
        if a7 then
            ba.Scale = self._fitScale or 1
            a8.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
            self:_fade(0, 0.3)
            ah(self.BodyStroke, { Transparency = 0 }, 0.3)
            ah(a9, { ImageTransparency = 0.6 }, 0.3)
        else
            a8.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 24)
            ah(ba, { Scale = self._fitScale or 1 }, 0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
            ah(a8, { Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5) }, 0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
            self:_fade(0, 0.35)
            ah(self.BodyStroke, { Transparency = 0 }, 0.35)
        end
        if not a7 then
            ah(a9, { ImageTransparency = 0.6 }, 0.5)
        end
        for bb, bc in ipairs(self.Tabs) do
            a6(bc._button, 0.1 + bb * 0.05, true)
        end
        for bb, bc in ipairs(self._tabSections or {}) do
            a6(bc, 0.06 + bb * 0.04, true)
        end
        self.Indicator.Visible = false
        task.delay(0.15 + #self.Tabs * 0.05, function()
            if self.CurrentTab then
                self:_placeIndicator(self.CurrentTab)
            end
        end)
    end

                end
            end
            ah(bu, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1) }, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
            task.delay(0.25, function()
                if not bg.Parent then
                    return
                end
                if a7._prompts then
                    by()
                    bA(a7._prompts, 1)
                else
                    bx()
                end
            end)
        end)
    end
    function a2:_fitToScreen(instant)
    local vp = self.Gui.AbsoluteSize
    if vp.X == 0 or vp.Y == 0 then
        return
    end
    local root = self.Root
    local cur = Vector2.new(root.Size.X.Offset, root.Size.Y.Offset)
    if not self._fitApplied or cur ~= self._fitApplied then
        self._wantSize = cur
    end
    local want = self._wantSize
    local size = cur
    if not self._resizeActive then
        local floorScale = E and 0.8 or 0.7
        local minSize = self._minSize or Vector2.zero
        size = Vector2.new(
            math.floor(math.max(math.min(want.X, (vp.X - 24) / floorScale), math.min(minSize.X, want.X))),
            math.floor(math.max(math.min(want.Y, (vp.Y - 24) / floorScale), math.min(minSize.Y, want.Y)))
        )
        if size ~= cur then
            root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size.X, size.Y)
        end
        self._fitApplied = size ~= want and size or nil
    end
    local base = (self._uiScale or 1) * (self._adaptive ~= false and k._adaptiveFactor(vp) or 1)
    local fit = math.min(base, (vp.X - 24) / math.max(size.X, 1), (vp.Y - 24) / math.max(size.Y, 1))
    self._fitScale = math.max(fit, 0.45)
    if self._introDone and self.Open then
        if instant then
            self.Scale.Scale = self._fitScale
        else
            ah(self.Scale, { Scale = self._fitScale }, 0.2)
            task.delay(0.22, function()
                if not self._destroyed then
                    self:_clampToScreen()
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end
    function k._adaptiveFactor(a7)
        if E then
            return 1
        end
        local a8 = math.min(a7.X / 1920, a7.Y / 1020)
        if a8 > 0.9 and a8 < 1.15 then
            return 1
        end
        return math.clamp(math.floor(a8 * 20 + 0.5) / 20, 0.85, 1.75)
    end
    function a2:SetAdaptiveSize(a7)
        self._adaptive = a7 ~= false
        self:_fitToScreen()
        task.delay(0.22, function()
            if not self._destroyed then
                self:_clampToScreen()
            end
        end)
    end
    function a2:GetAdaptiveSize()
        return self._adaptive ~= false
    end

    function k._spaced(a7, a8)
        if a7 <= 0 then
            return 0
        end
        return math.max(math.floor(a7 * a8 + (a8 - 1) * 4 + 0.5), 0)
    end
    function k._space(a7, a8)
        local a9 = k.DensityModes[k.Density] or 1
        if a7:IsA("UIListLayout") or a7:IsA("UIGridLayout") then
            a7:SetAttribute("Space", a8)
            a7.Padding = UDim.new(0, k._spaced(a8, a9))
        elseif a7:IsA("UIPadding") then
            a7:SetAttribute("SpaceL", a8[1])
            a7:SetAttribute("SpaceR", a8[2])
            a7:SetAttribute("SpaceT", a8[3])
            a7:SetAttribute("SpaceB", a8[4])
            a7.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, math.floor(a8[1] * math.sqrt(a9) + 0.5))
            a7.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, math.floor(a8[2] * math.sqrt(a9) + 0.5))
            a7.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, k._spaced(a8[3], a9))
            a7.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, k._spaced(a8[4], a9))
        end
        return a7
    end
    function k:SetDensity(a7)
        if type(a7) == "string" then
            for a8 in pairs(k.DensityModes) do
                if a8:lower() == a7:lower() then
                    a7 = a8
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if not k.DensityModes[a7] then
            a7 = "Default"
        end
        k.Density = a7
        for a8, a9 in ipairs(k.Windows) do
            if a9.Gui and a9.Gui.Parent then
                for ba, bb in ipairs(a9.Gui:GetDescendants()) do
                    local bc = bb:GetAttribute("Space")

                            bb,
                            {
                                bb:GetAttribute("SpaceL"),
                                bb:GetAttribute("SpaceR"),
                                bb:GetAttribute("SpaceT"),
                                bb:GetAttribute("SpaceB"),
                            }

        end

    end

        local function bd(be)
            ah(
                bc,
                { Color = be and z.Accent or z.Muted, Transparency = be and 0 or 0.55 },
                be and 0.15 or 0.3,
                Enum.EasingStyle.Quint
            )

        local be = 20
        local bf = false
        local bg = Vector2.zero
        local bh = Vector2.zero
        local bi = Vector2.zero

        local bk = Vector2.zero
        local bl = Vector2.zero

            return Vector2.new(a9.Size.X.Offset, a9.Size.Y.Offset)
        end
        local function bn()
            if a9.AnchorPoint == Vector2.zero then
                return
            end

                self._clampTween:Cancel()
                self._clampTween = nil
            end
            local bo = a9.AbsolutePosition - self.Gui.AbsolutePosition
            a9.AnchorPoint = Vector2.zero
            a9.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(bo.X, bo.Y)
        end
        local function bo()

            local bq = a9.AbsoluteSize / 2
            a9.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            a9.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(bp.X + bq.X, bp.Y + bq.Y)

            )
        end
        ba.InputBegan:Connect(function(bq)

                bn()

                    bf = false
                    bd(false)
                end

            if not self._resizeActive then
                return

            if bf then
                local br = (av() - bh) / self.Scale.Scale
                bk = bp(bg + br)
            end
            local br = math.min(bq, 1 / 30)
            local bs = math.exp(-be * br)
            local bt = bi - bk
            local bu = (bj + bt * be) * br
            bj = (bj - bu * be) * bs
            bi = bk + (bt + bu) * bs

            end
        end)
    end

    function a2:SetSize(a7, a8)
        if typeof(a7) == "UDim2" then

        if a7 == self:GetSize() then
            return
        end
        local bb = (a8 or not self.Root.Visible) and 0 or 0.3

    end
    local genv = (typeof(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv()) or _G or {}
    local safe_writefile = writefile or (syn and syn.write_file) or genv.writefile
    local safe_readfile = readfile or (syn and syn.read_file) or genv.readfile
    local safe_isfile = isfile or is_file or fileExists or file_exists or genv.isfile
    local safe_isfolder = isfolder or is_folder or folderExists or folder_exists or genv.isfolder
    local safe_makefolder = makefolder or make_folder or createfolder or create_folder or genv.makefolder
    local safe_delfile = delfile or deletefile or delete_file or removefile or remove_file or (genv and (genv.delfile or genv.deletefile))
    local safe_listfiles = listfiles or list_files or listdir or list_dir or genv.listfiles

    local function file_exists(path)
        if type(safe_isfile) == "function" then
            local ok, res = pcall(safe_isfile, path)
            if ok and type(res) == "boolean" then
                return res
            end
        end
        if type(safe_readfile) == "function" then
            local ok, content = pcall(safe_readfile, path)
            return ok and type(content) == "string"
        end
        return false
    end

    local function folder_exists(path)
        if type(safe_isfolder) == "function" then
            local ok, res = pcall(safe_isfolder, path)
            if ok and type(res) == "boolean" then
                return res
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function make_folder(path)
        if type(safe_makefolder) == "function" then
            pcall(safe_makefolder, path)
        end
    end

    local a7 = 1
    local a8 = "airflow:"
    local function a9(ba, bb)
        return ba.ConfigFolder .. "/" .. bb .. ".json"
    end
    local function ba()
        return type(safe_writefile) == "function" and type(safe_readfile) == "function"
    end
    local function bb(bc)
        pcall(function()
            local bd = ""
            for be in tostring(bc):gmatch("[^/\\]+") do
                bd = bd == "" and be or bd .. "/" .. be
                if not folder_exists(bd) then
                    make_folder(bd)
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    local function bc(bd)
        return string.format(
            "%02x%02x%02x",
            math.floor(bd.R * 255 + 0.5),
            math.floor(bd.G * 255 + 0.5),
            math.floor(bd.B * 255 + 0.5)
        )
    end
    local function bd(be)
        if type(be) == "table" then
            if type(be.Hex) == "string" then
                be = be.Hex
            elseif type(be[1]) == "number" then
                return Color3.new(be[1], be[2] or 0, be[3] or 0)
            end
        end
        if type(be) == "string" then
            local bf, bg, bh = be:match("^%s*#?(%x%x)(%x%x)(%x%x)%s*$")
            if bf then
                return Color3.fromRGB(tonumber(bf, 16), tonumber(bg, 16), tonumber(bh, 16))
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function be(bf)
        local bg = bf._type
        local bh = bf:Get()
        if type(bf._saveValue) == "function" then
            bh = bf:_saveValue()
        end
        if bg == "Keybind" then
            return { Type = bg, Value = bh and bh.Name or nil }
        elseif bg == "ColorPicker" then
            return { Type = bg, Value = typeof(bh) == "Color3" and { Hex = bc(bh) } or nil }
        end
        return { Type = bg, Value = bh }
    end
    local function bf(bg, bh, bi)
        local bj = bg._type
        local bk = bh.Value
        if bj == "Keybind" then
            bg:Set(type(bk) == "string" and Enum.KeyCode[bk] or nil, bi)
        elseif bj == "ColorPicker" then
            local bl = bd(bk)
            if bl then
                bg:Set(bl, bi)
            end
        elseif bj == "Dropdown" then
            bg:Set(bk, bi)
        elseif bj == "Toggle" then
            if type(bk) == "boolean" then
                bg:Set(bk, bi)
            end
        elseif bj == "Slider" or bj == "Stepper" or bj == "Progress" then
            local bl = tonumber(bk)
            if bl then
                bg:Set(bl, bi)
            end
        elseif bk ~= nil then
            bg:Set(bk, bi)
        end
    end
    local function bg(bh, bi)
        return type(bi) == "table" and (bi.Type == nil or bi.Type == bh._type)
    end
    local function bh(bi)
        local bj = {}
        if bi and bi._pendingFlags then
            for bk, bl in pairs(bi._pendingFlags) do
                bj[bk] = bl.Entry
            end
        end
        for bk, bl in pairs(k.Flags) do
            if bl._type and type(bl.Get) == "function" then
                local bm, bn = pcall(be, bl)
                if bm then
                    bj[bk] = bn
                end
            end
        end
        return bj
    end
    function a2:_applyFlags(bi, bj, strict)
        bj = bj == true
        self._pendingFlags = {}
        for bk, bl in pairs(bi) do
            local bm = k.Flags[bk]
            if bm and type(bm.Set) == "function" then
                if bg(bm, bl) then
                    pcall(bf, bm, bl, bj)
                end
            elseif type(bl) == "table" then
                self._pendingFlags[bk] = { Entry = bl, Silent = bj }
            end
        end
        if strict then
            for flag, control in pairs(k.Flags) do
                if bi[flag] == nil and control._defaultEntry and type(control.Set) == "function" then
                    pcall(bf, control, control._defaultEntry, bj)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    function a2:_flagCreated(bi, bj)
        local bk, bl = pcall(be, bj)
        if bk then
            bj._defaultEntry = bl
        end
        local bm = self._pendingFlags and self._pendingFlags[bi]
        if not bm then
            return
        end
        task.defer(function()
            if self._pendingFlags[bi] ~= bm or k.Flags[bi] ~= bj or bj._destroyed then
                return
            end
            self._pendingFlags[bi] = nil
            if bg(bj, bm.Entry) then
                pcall(bf, bj, bm.Entry, bm.Silent)
            end
        end)
    end
    function a2:_configChanged()
        for bi, bj in ipairs(self._configListeners) do
            aJ(bj)
        end
    end
    function a2:OnConfigChanged(bi)
        table.insert(self._configListeners, bi)
        return function()
            local bj = table.find(self._configListeners, bi)
            if bj then
                table.remove(self._configListeners, bj)
            end
        end
    end
    local function bi(bj)
        if type(bj) ~= "string" or bj == "" or #bj > 64 then
            return false
        end
        if bj:find('[\\/:%*%?"<>|%c]') or bj == "." or bj == ".." then
            return false
        end
        local up = bj:upper()
        if up == "CON" or up == "PRN" or up == "AUX" or up == "NUL" or up:match("^COM%d$") or up:match("^LPT%d$") then
            return false
        end
        return true
    end
    local function bj(bk, bl, bm)
        return e:JSONEncode({ Folder = bk.ConfigFolder, Name = bl, Version = a7, Config = e:JSONEncode(bm) })
    end
    local bk = [[ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/]]
    local bl = {}
    for bm = 1, #bk do
        bl[bk:byte(bm)] = bm - 1
    end
    local function bm(bn)
        if #bn % 4 ~= 0 then
            return nil
        end
        local bo = {}
        for bp = 1, #bn, 4 do
            local bq, br = 0, 0
            for bs = 0, 3 do
                local bt = bn:byte(bp + bs)
                local bu = bl[bt]
                if bt == 61 and bp + 3 >= #bn then
                    br += 1
                    bu = 0
                elseif bu == nil or br > 0 then
                    return nil
                end
                bq = bq * 64 + bu
            end
            if br > 2 then
                return nil
            end
            local bs = string.char(bit32.extract(bq, 16, 8), bit32.extract(bq, 8, 8), bit32.extract(bq, 0, 8))
            table.insert(bo, bs:sub(1, 3 - br))
        end
        return table.concat(bo)
    end
    local function bn(bo)
        bo = tostring(bo or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        if bo:sub(1, #a8) == a8 then
            bo = bm((bo:sub(#a8 + 1):gsub("%s", "")))
            if not bo then
                return nil, "invalid config code"
            end
        end
        local bp, bq = pcall(function()
            return e:JSONDecode(bo)
        end)
        if not bp or type(bq) ~= "table" then
            return nil, "invalid config code"
        end
        local br = {}
        if type(bq.Config) == "string" or (type(bq.Config) == "table" and bq.Config.Type == nil) then
            br.Folder = type(bq.Folder) == "string" and bq.Folder or nil
            br.Name = type(bq.Name) == "string" and bq.Name or nil
            br.Version = tonumber(bq.Version)
            local bs = bq.Config
            if type(bs) == "string" then
                bp, bs = pcall(function()
                    return e:JSONDecode(bq.Config)
                end)
                if not bp then
                    return nil, "invalid config code"
                end
            end
            if type(bs) ~= "table" then
                return nil, "invalid config code"
            end
            bq = bs
        end
        for bs, bt in pairs(bq) do
            if type(bs) == "string" then
                if type(bt) ~= "table" then
                    bq[bs] = { Value = bt }
                end
            end
        end
        return bq, br
    end
    local function bo(bp, bq, br)
        if not bi(bq) then
            return false, "invalid config name"
        end
        if not ba() then
            return false, "file API unavailable"
        end
        local bs, bt = pcall(bj, bp, bq, br)
        if not bs then
            return false, "could not encode config"
        end
        bb(bp.ConfigFolder)
        local path = a9(bp, bq)
        local written, writeErr = pcall(safe_writefile, path, bt)
        if not written then
            return false, writeErr
        end
        return true
    end
    local function bp(bq, br)
        if not ba() then
            return nil, "file API unavailable"
        end
        if not bi(br) then
            return nil, "invalid config name"
        end
        local bs = a9(bq, br)
        local bt, bu = pcall(safe_readfile, bs)
        if not bt or type(bu) ~= "string" then
            return nil, "no config named " .. tostring(br)
        end
        local bv, bw = bn(bu)
        if not bv then
            return nil, "config is not valid JSON"
        end
        return bv, bw
    end
    function a2:ConfigExists(bq)
        return bi(bq) and ba() and file_exists(a9(self, bq)) or false
    end
    function a2:SaveConfig(bq)
        bq = bq or self.ConfigName
        if not bq then
            return false, "no config name"
        end
        local data = bh(self)
        local br, bs = bo(self, bq, data)
        if br then
            self.ConfigName = bq
            local snapOk, snap = pcall(a2._stable, data)
            if snapOk then
                self._savedSnapshot = snap
                self._snapshotStale = false
            else
                self._snapshotStale = true
            end
        end
        return br, bs
    end
    function a2:LoadConfig(bq, br, strict)
        bq = bq or self.ConfigName
        if not bq then
            return false, "no config name"
        end
        local bs, bt = bp(self, bq)
        if not bs then
            return false, bt
        end
        if strict == nil then
            strict = self.StrictLoad == true
        end
        self:_applyFlags(bs, br, strict)
        self.ConfigName = bq
        self.LoadedConfig = bq
        self._snapshotStale = true
        self:_configChanged()
        return true
    end
    function a2:DeleteConfig(bq)
        if not ba() or type(delfile) ~= "function" then
            return false, "file API unavailable"
        end
        if not bi(bq) then
            return false, "invalid config name"
        end
        local br = a9(self, bq)
        if not isfile(br) then
            return false, "no config named " .. bq
        end
        local bs, bt = pcall(delfile, br)
        if not bs then
            return false, bt
        end
        if self.LoadedConfig == bq then
            self.LoadedConfig = nil
        end
        if self.ConfigName == bq then
            self.ConfigName = nil
        end
        for _, mode in ipairs({ "Global", "Account" }) do
            if self:GetAutoload(mode) == bq then
                self:SetAutoload(nil, mode)
            end
        end
        self:_configChanged()
        return true
    end
    function a2:RenameConfig(bq, br)
        if not bi(br) then
            return false, "invalid config name"
        end
        if bq == br then
            return true
        end
        if self:ConfigExists(br) then
            return false, br .. " already exists"
        end
        local bs, bt = bp(self, bq)
        if not bs then
            return false, bt
        end
        if type(delfile) ~= "function" then
            return false, "rename needs delfile"
        end
        local bu
        bu, bt = bo(self, br, bs)
        if not bu then
            return false, bt
        end
        local removed, removeErr = pcall(delfile, a9(self, bq))
        if not removed or isfile(a9(self, bq)) then
            pcall(delfile, a9(self, br))
            return false, removed and ("could not remove " .. bq) or tostring(removeErr)
        end
        if self.LoadedConfig == bq then
            self.LoadedConfig = br
        end
        if self.ConfigName == bq then
            self.ConfigName = br
        end
        for bv, bw in ipairs({ "Global", "Account" }) do
            if self:GetAutoload(bw) == bq then
                self:SetAutoload(br, bw)
            end
        end
        self:_configChanged()
        return true
    end
    function a2:DuplicateConfig(src, dst)
        if not bi(dst) then
            return false, "invalid config name"
        end
        if self:ConfigExists(dst) then
            return false, dst .. " already exists"
        end
        local data, err = bp(self, src)
        if not data then
            return false, err
        end
        local ok, saveErr = bo(self, dst, data)
        if not ok then
            return false, saveErr
        end
        self:_configChanged()
        return true
    end
    function a2:ListConfigs()
        local bq = {}
        if type(listfiles) ~= "function" or type(isfolder) ~= "function" or not isfolder(self.ConfigFolder) then
            return bq
        end
        local br, bs = pcall(listfiles, self.ConfigFolder)
        if not br or type(bs) ~= "table" then
            return bq
        end
        for bt, bu in ipairs(bs) do
            local bv = bu:match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
            if bv then
                table.insert(bq, bv)
            end
        end
        table.sort(bq, function(bt, bu)
            return bt:lower() < bu:lower()
        end)
        return bq
    end
    function a2:ResetConfig(bq)
        self._pendingFlags = {}
        self._snapshotStale = true
        for br, bs in pairs(k.Flags) do
            if bs._defaultEntry and type(bs.Set) == "function" then
                pcall(bf, bs, bs._defaultEntry, bq == true)
            end
        end
        self:_configChanged()
        return true
    end
    local function bq(br)
        if type(isfolder) ~= "function" or not isfolder(br) then
            return true
        end
        if type(delfolder) == "function" and pcall(delfolder, br) and not isfolder(br) then
            return true
        end
        if type(listfiles) == "function" then
            local bs, bt = pcall(listfiles, br)
            for bu, bv in ipairs(bs and bt or {}) do
                if isfolder(bv) then
                    bq(bv)
                elseif type(delfile) == "function" then
                    pcall(delfile, bv)
                end
            end
        end
        if type(delfolder) == "function" then
            pcall(delfolder, br)
        end
        return not isfolder(br)
    end
    function a2:ClearWorkspace()
        local folder = tostring(self.ConfigFolder or "")
        local safeFolder = folder ~= ""
            and folder ~= "."
            and not folder:find("%.%.")
            and not folder:find("^[/\\]")
            and not folder:find(":")
        local br = safeFolder and bq(folder) or false
        self.LoadedConfig = nil
        self.ConfigName = nil
        self._savedSnapshot = nil
        self._snapshotStale = true
        for bs, bt in ipairs(self._workspaceListeners or {}) do
            aJ(bt)
        end
        self:_configChanged()
        return br
    end
    function a2:ExportConfig(br, asCode)
        local bs, bt
        if br then
            bs, bt = bp(self, br)
            if not bs then
                return nil, bt
            end
        else
            bs = bh(self)
            br = self.LoadedConfig or self.ConfigName
        end
        local bu, bv = pcall(bj, self, br, bs)
        if not bu then
            return nil, "could not encode config"
        end
        if asCode == true then
            return a8 .. a2._b64encode(bv)
        end
        return bv
    end
    function a2:DecodeConfig(br)
        return bn(br)
    end
    function a2:ImportConfig(br, bs, bt)
        local bu, bv = bn(br)
        if not bu then
            return false, bv
        end
        if not bt and bv.Folder and bv.Folder ~= self.ConfigFolder then
            return false, "config is for " .. bv.Folder
        end
        if bs == true then
            bs = bv.Name
            if not bi(bs) then
                return false, "config has no usable name"
            end
        end
        if bs then
            local bw, bx = bo(self, bs, bu)
            if not bw then
                return false, bx
            end
        end
        self:_applyFlags(bu, false, self.StrictLoad == true)
        self._snapshotStale = true
        if bs then
            self.ConfigName = bs
            self.LoadedConfig = bs
        end
        self:_configChanged()
        return true, nil, bs
    end
    local function br(bs)
        if not ba() then
            return nil
        end
        local bt, bu = pcall(safe_readfile, bs)
        if bt and type(bu) == "string" and bu ~= "" then
            return bu
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function bs(bt)
        if ba() and type(safe_delfile) == "function" and file_exists(bt) then
            pcall(safe_delfile, bt)
        end
    end
    local function bt(bu)
        return bu.ConfigFolder .. "/configsettings.txt"
    end
    function a2:_readConfigSettings()
        local bu = br(bt(self))
        if bu then
            local bv, bw = pcall(function()
                return e:JSONDecode(bu)
            end)
            if bv and type(bw) == "table" then
                return bw
            end
        end
        return {}
    end
    function a2:_writeConfigSettings(bu)
        if not ba() then
            return false, "file API unavailable"
        end
        local bv = self:_readConfigSettings()
        for bw, bx in pairs(bu) do
            bv[bw] = bx
        end
        bb(self.ConfigFolder)
        return pcall(safe_writefile, bt(self), e:JSONEncode(bv))
    end
    local function bu(bv, bw)
        if bw == "Account" then
            local plr = j or f.LocalPlayer
            local uid = (plr and plr.UserId) or (f.LocalPlayer and f.LocalPlayer.UserId) or 0
            return bv.ConfigFolder .. "/autoload_" .. tostring(uid) .. ".txt"
        end
        return bv.ConfigFolder .. "/autoload.txt"
    end
    function a2:GetAutoloadMode()
        return self:_readConfigSettings().AutoloadMode == "Account" and "Account" or "Global"
    end
    function a2:GetAutoload(bv)
        local function readName(mode)
            local raw = br(bu(self, mode))
            raw = raw and raw:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
            if raw and bi(raw) then
                return raw
            end
            return nil
        end
        if bv then
            return readName(bv)
        end
        local settings = self:_readConfigSettings()
        if settings.AutoloadDisabled == true then
            return nil
        end
        local name = readName("Account") or readName("Global")
        if not name and self.ConfigName and self:ConfigExists(self.ConfigName) then
            return self.ConfigName
        end
        return name
    end
    function a2:SetAutoload(bv, bw)
        if not ba() then
            return false, "file API unavailable"
        end
        if bv ~= nil and bv ~= "" and not bi(bv) then
            return false, "invalid config name"
        end
        bw = bw or self:GetAutoloadMode()
        local bx, by = true, nil
        if bv == nil or bv == "" then
            bs(bu(self, bw))
            if bw == "Global" then
                bs(bu(self, "Account"))
            end
            self:_writeConfigSettings({ AutoloadDisabled = true })
        else
            bb(self.ConfigFolder)
            bx, by = pcall(safe_writefile, bu(self, bw), tostring(bv))
            if bx then
                if bw == "Global" then
                    bs(bu(self, "Account"))
                end
                self:_writeConfigSettings({ AutoloadDisabled = false })
            end
        end
        self:_configChanged()
        return bx, by
    end
    function a2:SetAutoloadMode(bv)
        bv = bv == "Account" and "Account" or "Global"
        if bv == self:GetAutoloadMode() then
            return true
        end
        local bw = self:GetAutoload()
        local bx, by = self:_writeConfigSettings({ AutoloadMode = bv })
        if not bx then
            return false, by
        end
        bs(bu(self, bv == "Account" and "Global" or "Account"))
        if bw then
            return self:SetAutoload(bw, bv)
        end
        self:_configChanged()
        return true
    end
    function a2:LoadAutoload(bv)
        local bw = self:GetAutoload() or (self.ConfigName and self:ConfigExists(self.ConfigName) and self.ConfigName)
        if not bw then
            return false, "no autoload config"
        end
        return self:LoadConfig(bw, bv)
    end
    function a2._stable(value, depth)
        depth = depth or 0
        local kind = type(value)
        if kind == "table" and depth < 8 then
            local keys = {}
            for key in pairs(value) do
                keys[#keys + 1] = key
            end
            table.sort(keys, function(x, y)
                return tostring(x) < tostring(y)
            end)
            local parts = {}
            for _, key in ipairs(keys) do
                parts[#parts + 1] = tostring(key) .. "=" .. a2._stable(value[key], depth + 1)
            end
            return "{" .. table.concat(parts, ",") .. "}"
        end
        return kind .. ":" .. tostring(value)
    end
    function a2._b64encode(data)
        local alphabet = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
        local out = {}
        for i = 1, #data, 3 do
            local b1, b2, b3 = data:byte(i, i + 2)
            local n = b1 * 65536 + (b2 or 0) * 256 + (b3 or 0)
            local c1 = bit32.extract(n, 18, 6) + 1
            local c2 = bit32.extract(n, 12, 6) + 1
            local c3 = bit32.extract(n, 6, 6) + 1
            local c4 = bit32.extract(n, 0, 6) + 1
            out[#out + 1] = alphabet:sub(c1, c1)
                .. alphabet:sub(c2, c2)
                .. (b2 and alphabet:sub(c3, c3) or "=")
                .. (b3 and alphabet:sub(c4, c4) or "=")
        end
        return table.concat(out)
    end
    function a2:_autoSaveTick()
        local name = self.LoadedConfig or self.ConfigName
        if not name or not bi(name) or not ba() then
            return
        end
        local ok, snapshot = pcall(function()
            return a2._stable(bh(self))
        end)
        if not ok then
            return
        end
        if self._snapshotStale or self._savedSnapshot == nil then
            self._savedSnapshot = snapshot
            self._snapshotStale = false
            return
        end
        if snapshot ~= self._savedSnapshot and self:SaveConfig(name) then
            self._savedSnapshot = snapshot
        end
    end
    function a2:GetAutoSave()
        return self._autoSave == true
    end
    function a2:SetAutoSave(enabled, interval)
        self._autoSave = enabled == true
        self._autoSaveInterval = math.clamp(tonumber(interval) or self._autoSaveInterval or 3, 1, 60)
        if self._autoSave then
            self._snapshotStale = true
        end
        if self._autoSave and not self._autoSaveRunning then
            self._autoSaveRunning = true
            task.spawn(function()
                while not self._destroyed and self._autoSave do
                    task.wait(self._autoSaveInterval)
                    if self._destroyed or not self._autoSave then
                        break
                    end
                    pcall(self._autoSaveTick, self)
                end
                self._autoSaveRunning = false
            end)
        end
        return true
    end
    k._themeListeners = {}
    local function bv(bw)
        if typeof(bw) == "Color3" then
            return bw
        elseif type(bw) == "string" then
            local bx, by, bz = bw:match("^%s*#?(%x%x)(%x%x)(%x%x)%s*$")
            if bx then
                return Color3.fromRGB(tonumber(bx, 16), tonumber(by, 16), tonumber(bz, 16))
            end
        elseif type(bw) == "table" then
            local bx, by, bz = bw[1] or bw.R, bw[2] or bw.G, bw[3] or bw.B
            if tonumber(bx) and tonumber(by) and tonumber(bz) then
                return Color3.fromRGB(bx, by, bz)
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function bw(bx)
        return string.format(
            "#%02X%02X%02X",
            math.floor(bx.R * 255 + 0.5),
            math.floor(bx.G * 255 + 0.5),
            math.floor(bx.B * 255 + 0.5)
        )
    end
    k._toneTags = {}
    for bx, by in ipairs(n) do
        k._toneTags[string.lower(by)] = by
    end
    function k._color(bx)
        if type(bx) == "string" then
            local by = k._toneTags[string.lower(bx)]
            if by then
                return z[by]
            end
        end
        return bv(bx)
    end
    function k._rich(bx)
        bx = tostring(bx == nil and "" or bx)
        if not string.find(bx, "<", 1, true) then
            return bx
        end
        return (
            string.gsub(bx, "<(/?)(%a+%d*)>", function(by, bz)
                local bA = k._toneTags[string.lower(bz)]
                if not bA then
                    return nil
                end
                if by == "/" then
                    return "</font>"
                end
                return '<font color="' .. bw(z[bA]) .. '">'
            end)
        )
    end
    function k.Colorize(...)
        local bx = { ... }
        if bx[1] == k then
            table.remove(bx, 1)
        end
        local by, bz = tostring(bx[1] == nil and "" or bx[1]), bx[2]
        local bA = type(bz) == "string" and k._toneTags[string.lower(bz)]
        if bA then
            return "<" .. bA .. ">" .. by .. "</" .. bA .. ">"
        end
        local bB = bv(bz)
        if not bB then
            return by
        end
        return '<font color="' .. bw(bB) .. '">' .. by .. "</font>"
    end
    function k:SetCornerRadius(bx, by)
        bx = math.clamp(math.floor((tonumber(bx) or 10) + 0.5), 0, 20)
        k.Roundness = bx / 10
        for bz, bA in pairs(k._cornerBases) do
            if not bz.Parent then
                k._cornerBases[bz] = nil
            else
                local bB = UDim.new(0, math.floor(bA * k.Roundness + 0.5))
                if by then
                    bz.CornerRadius = bB
                else
                    ah(bz, { CornerRadius = bB }, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
                end
            end
        end
        return bx
    end
    function k:GetCornerRadius()
        return math.floor(k.Roundness * 10 + 0.5)
    end
    function k:SetTheme(bx, by)
        if type(bx) == "string" then
            local bz = k.ThemePresets[bx]
            if not bz then
                return false
            end
            k.ThemeName = bx
            bx = bz
        elseif type(bx) ~= "table" then
            return false
        end
        local bz = false
        for bA, bB in ipairs(n) do
            local bC = bv(bx[bB])
            if bC and bC ~= z[bB] then
                z[bB] = bC
                bz = true
            end
        end
        if not bz then
            return true
        end
        _()
        Y()
        af(by and 0 or 0.3)
        for bA, bB in pairs(S) do
            if bA.Parent then
                pcall(bB)
            end
        end
        for bA, bB in ipairs(k._themeListeners) do
            pcall(bB)
        end
        return true
    end
    function k:GetTheme()
        local bx = {}
        for by, bz in ipairs(n) do
            bx[bz] = z[bz]
        end
        return bx
    end
    function k:SetDefaultTheme(bx)
        k.DefaultTheme = bx
        if bx == nil then
            return true
        end
        local by = false
        for bz, bA in ipairs(k.Windows) do
            if not bA._destroyed then
                if bA:GetDefaultTheme() then
                    return true
                end
                by = true
            end
        end
        return k:SetTheme(bx, not by)
    end
    function k:GetDefaultTheme()
        return k.DefaultTheme
    end
    local function bx(by)
        return by.ConfigFolder .. "/themes"
    end
    function a2:SaveTheme(by)
        if not ba() then
            return false, "file API unavailable"
        end
        if type(by) ~= "string" or by == "" then
            return false, "theme needs a name"
        end
        bb(self.ConfigFolder)
        bb(bx(self))
        local bz = {}
        for bA, bB in ipairs(n) do
            bz[bB] = bw(z[bB])
        end
        return pcall(function()
            writefile(bx(self) .. "/" .. by .. ".json", e:JSONEncode(bz))
        end)
    end
    function a2:LoadTheme(by, bz)
        if k.ThemePresets[by] then
            return k:SetTheme(by, bz)
        end
        local bA = br(bx(self) .. "/" .. tostring(by) .. ".json")
        if not bA then
            return false, "no theme named " .. tostring(by)
        end
        local bB, bC = pcall(function()
            return e:JSONDecode(bA)
        end)
        if not bB or type(bC) ~= "table" then
            return false, "theme is not valid JSON"
        end
        k.ThemeName = by
        return k:SetTheme(bC, bz)
    end
    function a2:DeleteTheme(by)
        if not ba() or type(delfile) ~= "function" then
            return false, "file API unavailable"
        end
        local bz = bx(self) .. "/" .. tostring(by) .. ".json"
        if not isfile(bz) then
            return false, "no theme named " .. tostring(by)
        end
        delfile(bz)
        if self:GetDefaultTheme() == by then
            self:SetDefaultTheme(nil)
        end
        return true
    end
    function a2:ListThemes()
        local by = {}
        if type(listfiles) ~= "function" or type(isfolder) ~= "function" or not isfolder(bx(self)) then
            return by
        end
        for bz, bA in ipairs(listfiles(bx(self))) do
            local bB = bA:match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
            if bB then
                table.insert(by, bB)
            end
        end
        table.sort(by)
        return by
    end
    function a2:GetDefaultTheme()
        return br(bx(self) .. "/default.txt")
    end
    function a2:SetDefaultTheme(by)
        if not ba() then
            return false, "file API unavailable"
        end
        bb(self.ConfigFolder)
        bb(bx(self))
        local bz = bx(self) .. "/default.txt"
        if by == nil or by == "" then
            if isfile(bz) and type(delfile) == "function" then
                pcall(delfile, bz)
            end
            return true
        end
        return pcall(writefile, bz, tostring(by))
    end
    function a2:_applyDefaultTheme()
        local by = self:GetDefaultTheme()
        if by then
            local bz, bA = pcall(self.LoadTheme, self, by, true)
            if bz and bA then
                return
            end
        end
        if k.DefaultTheme ~= nil then
            pcall(k.SetTheme, k, k.DefaultTheme, true)
        end
    end
    k.BackgroundPresets = {
        ["Deep Violet"] = "rbxassetid://136310484943077",
        ["Blood Red"] = "rbxassetid://121343473918667",
        ["Cyanic"] = "rbxassetid://95656189244173",
        ["Amber Glow"] = "rbxassetid://107795771598485",
        ["Bloomings"] = "rbxassetid://133541508207801",
        ["Lavender Pink"] = "rbxassetid://126107479485287",
    }
    k.BackgroundPresetOrder = { "Deep Violet", "Blood Red", "Cyanic", "Amber Glow", "Bloomings", "Lavender Pink" }
    k._imageExtensions = { png = true, jpg = true, jpeg = true, webp = true, gif = true, bmp = true }
    function a2:_backgroundFolder()
        return self.ConfigFolder .. "/backgrounds"
    end
    function a2:_trimBackground(by)
        return (tostring(by or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$"))
    end
    function a2:_resolveBackground(by)
        by = self:_trimBackground(by)
        local bz = k.BackgroundPresets[by]
        if bz then
            return bz
        end
        if by:match("^%d+$") then
            return "rbxassetid://" .. by
        end
        if
            by:match("^rbxassetid://%d+")
            or by:match("^rbxasset://")
            or by:match("^rbxthumb://")
            or by:match("^https?://www%.roblox%.com/asset")
        then
            return by
        end
        local bA = ba() and typeof(getcustomasset) == "function"
        if by:match("^https?://") then
            if not bA then
                return nil, "web images need writefile and getcustomasset"
            end
            local bB = by:match("^[^?#]+") or by
            local bC = (bB:match("%.(%w+)$") or "png"):lower()
            if not k._imageExtensions[bC] then
                bC = "png"
            end
            local bD = (bB:gsub("^https?://", ""):gsub("[^%w]", "_")):sub(-60)
            local bE = self:_backgroundFolder() .. "/cache"
            local bF = bE .. "/" .. bD .. "_" .. #by .. "." .. bC
            if not isfile(bF) then
                local bG, bH = pcall(function()
                    local bG = (syn and syn.request) or http_request or request
                    if bG then
                        local bH = bG({ Url = by, Method = "GET" })
                        return bH and bH.Body
                    end
                    return game:HttpGet(by, true)
                end)
                if not bG or type(bH) ~= "string" or #bH < 128 then
                    return nil, "could not download the image"
                end
                local bI = bH:sub(1, 64):lower()
                if bI:find("<!doctype") or bI:find("<html") then
                    return nil, "that link is a web page, not an image"
                end
                bb(bE)
                if not pcall(writefile, bF, bH) then
                    return nil, "could not save the image"
                end
            end
            local bG, bH = pcall(getcustomasset, bF)
            if bG and type(bH) == "string" and bH ~= "" then
                return bH
            end
            pcall(delfile, bF)
            return nil, "the downloaded file is not an image"
        end
        if bA then
            for bB, bC in ipairs({ self:_backgroundFolder() .. "/" .. by, by }) do
                if isfile(bC) then
                    local bD, bE = pcall(getcustomasset, bC)
                    if bD and type(bE) == "string" and bE ~= "" then
                        return bE
                    end
                    return nil, "could not load " .. by
                end
            end
        end
        return nil, "no image called " .. by
    end
    function a2:SetBackground(by)
        self._backgroundGeneration += 1
        local bz = self._backgroundGeneration
        local bA = self._backgroundImage
        by = by ~= false and self:_trimBackground(by) or ""
        if by == "" or by == "None" then
            self.Background = nil
            ah(bA, { ImageTransparency = 1 }, 0.25)
            task.delay(0.25, function()
                if self._backgroundGeneration == bz then
                    bA.Visible = false
                    bA.Image = ""
                end
            end)
            self:SetTransparent(self.Transparent)
            return true
        end
        local bB, bC = self:_resolveBackground(by)
        if self._backgroundGeneration ~= bz or self._destroyed then
            return false, "replaced by a newer background"
        end
        if not bB then
            return false, bC
        end
        self.Background = by
        bA.Image = bB
        bA.Visible = true
        ah(bA, { ImageTransparency = self.BackgroundTransparency }, 0.3)
        self:SetTransparent(self.Transparent)
        return true
    end
    function a2:GetBackground()
        return self.Background
    end
    function a2:SetBackgroundTransparency(by)
        self.BackgroundTransparency = math.clamp(tonumber(by) or 0.35, 0, 1)
        if self.Background then
            ah(self._backgroundImage, { ImageTransparency = self.BackgroundTransparency }, 0.15)
        end
    end
    function a2:ListBackgrounds()
        local by = table.clone(k.BackgroundPresetOrder)
        local bz = self:_backgroundFolder()
        if type(listfiles) == "function" and type(isfolder) == "function" and isfolder(bz) then
            local bA = {}
            for bB, bC in ipairs(listfiles(bz)) do
                local bD = bC:match("([^/]+)$")
                local bE = bD and bD:match("%.(%w+)$")
                if bE and k._imageExtensions[bE:lower()] then
                    table.insert(bA, bD)
                end
            end
            table.sort(bA)
            for bB, bC in ipairs(bA) do
                table.insert(by, bC)
            end
        end
        return by
    end

    local function bz(bA, bB)
        bB = bB or 0
        bA.GroupTransparency = 1
        bA.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, bB + 14)
